# Site Traceability Matrix

**Last reviewed:** unknown · **Owner:** TBD

## How to read this

This document has one section per area, in navigation order. Each area carries a Checklist
table, what gets ticked, and a Chain table, where that element's data comes from. Gates are
defined in this document's Gates section. The element ID is the join to the UI spec.

```bash
# whole document; run between two "## NN ·" headings for one area
awk -F'|' '$2 ~ /^ *[A-Z]+-[0-9]+ *$/ && $4 ~ /\[[ x]\]/ {
  n++; s+=($4~/x/); t+=($5~/x/); v+=($6~/x/); te+=($7~/x/)
} END { print n, s, t, v, te }' docs/product/03-SITE-TRACEABILITY.md
```

## Gates

**S, Specified.** The element's entry under UI-SCREENS.md exists with acceptance criteria a
test can be written from, its states, and its data contract. Evidence: the entry's anchor.

**T, Traced.** The Chain row is complete and every citation resolves: endpoint in the vendored
OpenAPI snapshot, handler file and line, tables in `gcp/schema.sql`, producer job and
schedule in `gcp/deploy.sh`, test files. `none` is allowed only with a reason. Evidence: the
date and commit of the verification.

**V, Validated.** The element was observed working against real data through a production
path: a recorded request and response on staging, a `scripts/db_query_cr.sh` result showing
the rows the page needs, or a replay flag (`REPLAY_DATE`, `BRIEF_AS_OF`, `INSIGHT_AS_OF`) for
pipeline-produced data. Reading the code never counts (CLAUDE.md §3.11). Evidence: a link to
the PR or issue comment holding the command and its output, with the date.

**Te, Tested.** Automated tests cover the element and each of its states at every layer the
chain crosses, they pass, and the suite they belong to runs on pull requests in its repo.
Today: stocks pytest runs in the Backtest Pipeline workflow on pull requests to `main`;
solyra Vitest and solyra Playwright both run on pull requests and pushes to `main` in solyra
CI (solyra [.github/workflows/ci.yml](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/.github/workflows/ci.yml)'s `checks` and `e2e` jobs). A Playwright-only element can tick
Te citing a CI run of the `e2e` job, once the specific test that covers the element and state
has actually run there. Evidence: test file links and a CI run link.

Rules:

- V requires T. Te requires S. S and T are independent.
- A tick is a claim with an evidence entry and date in the same commit.
- Nothing un-ticks automatically. The registry drift check queues the document when its
  declared code paths move; the flagged area's V and Te are then re-earned, not carried.

### Backend notes
- Inherited by every row below (not repeated per row): project `adept-mountain-474619-d4`, region `us-east1`, service account `trading-runner@adept-mountain-474619-d4.iam.gserviceaccount.com` for every Cloud Run job, image `us-east1-docker.pkg.dev/adept-mountain-474619-d4/trading/trading-system` (the `IMAGE_REF` every `deploy_*` function in [gcp/deploy.sh](../../gcp/deploy.sh) references), the Cloud SQL connection path `gcp/database.py:93` `get_engine()` (Cloud SQL Python Connector, `CLOUD_SQL_CONNECTION_NAME` + `DB_USER`/`DB_PASS`/`DB_NAME`), and Lovable as the SPA host serving every solyra route (SHARED-03's data path exists specifically to re-point `/api/*` off Lovable's static hosting). The API service itself runs as a separate account, `trading-platform-svc@`, set by `--service-account` in [platform/deploy.sh](../../platform/deploy.sh); jobs and the API service are not the same identity.
- Auth is global ASGI middleware, not a per-router dependency (`auth_middleware` wraps every request; `_path_requires_auth` decides per-path). It only verifies a bearer token when `AUTH_MODE=firebase`; `iap` and `open` call through unconditionally.
- Freshness has two independent paths that must be read separately: the standalone `freshness-watchdog` Cloud Run job (hourly during RTH, once after close) that exits non-zero into the failure lane on a stale table, and the on-demand `/api/health/freshness` endpoint, which wraps the same `scripts/audit_data_freshness.py` module behind a 5-minute in-process cache. No solyra component calls `/api/health/freshness` directly today (see the SHARED-07 gap below); the same cached report reaches the UI through the sibling `GET /api/admin/data-sources` endpoint (`platform/api/routers/admin.py:1391-1402`), which imports and shares `freshness_report_dict()`.
- Design drift carried from the pre-#957 architecture diagrams, per spec §4.5: the dashboard was labelled "no per-user auth" and the platform service "FastAPI + React" in the Claude Design docs. Both predate the #957 split (the SPA moved to this solyra repo; `platform/` now serves the API only, and every `/api/*` route is subject to the `AUTH_MODE` posture described in SHARED-01) and are stale as design labels, not as a statement about the current code.

### Gaps
- 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md's Cloud Run job inventory table marks the Secrets column absent (its em-dash placeholder) for `fetch-market-data`, `backfill-daily-indicators`, `fetch-premarket-refresh`, `premarket-brief` and `fetch-top-movers` (cited from Dashboard's Data column, area 04). Reading the `deploy_*` functions directly shows each references `${DB_SECRET_FLAG}`, which expands (`_build_secret_flag`, [gcp/deploy.sh:1038](../../gcp/deploy.sh)) to `DB_PASS=db-trading-pass:latest,AV_API_KEY=av-api-key:latest,ALPHA_VANTAGE_API_KEY=av-api-key:latest,DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL=discord-webhook-insights:latest` plus optional Discord/FRED/Benzinga pairs. The generated table's parser evidently does not resolve the `${DB_SECRET_FLAG}` variable reference to a literal `--set-secrets`, so it undercounts secrets for every job that uses the shared helper rather than an inline flag. Not fixed here: 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md is a generated doc (see its own Maintenance column).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` markers were found in any handler this task's areas cite (`platform/api/auth.py`, `platform/api/main.py`, `platform/api/routers/dashboard.py`, `platform/api/routers/config.py`, `platform/api/routers/health.py`, `platform/api/routers/live.py`, `platform/api/routers/catalysts.py`, `platform/api/routers/signals.py`, `platform/api/routers/playbook.py`, `platform/api/routers/waitlist.py`), and every endpoint cited from these five areas carries a `response_model`. The "66 of 98 validated trivially" untyped-response gap (spec §4.5) does not land on any row in SHARED, Landing, AuthGate, AppShell or Dashboard.
- SHARED-07: the design seed for these Backend notes stated the on-demand `/api/health/freshness` endpoint is polled by a Dashboard widget, echoing the same claim in `platform/api/routers/health.py`'s own module docstring ("shared by the CLI and the Dashboard widget"). No such widget exists in solyra today: a grep of `src/` for `health/freshness` finds no caller, and solyra [tests/dashboard/data-pipeline-widget.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/dashboard/data-pipeline-widget.spec.ts) is a regression test proving the old `DataPipelineStatus` widget's absence since the stocks/solyra split (`stocks#957`). The freshness data that does reach the UI arrives through the sibling `GET /api/admin/data-sources` endpoint on the Admin page (area 14), which shares the same `freshness_report_dict()` cache. Corrected in this task, 2026-09-28.
- **`/api/health/freshness` blocks over 30 seconds before answering 503.** A live probe of the staging endpoint on 2026-09-28 timed out at 30 seconds on its first request, then answered 503 twice more in 0.5 seconds each with "Freshness audit in progress and no cached report is available yet. Retry shortly." (filed as [stocks#1224](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1224)).

### Backend notes
- Landing is the one area with no pipeline behind it: every element except the waitlist form is static JSX plus bundled fixtures (`src/components/landing/fixtures.ts` for Hero/ChartShowcase); nothing here reads Cloud SQL or waits on a Cloud Run job.
- The page must render with zero runtime-config or Firebase cost (issue #26): `ConfigGate` only wraps the gated app group and `/auth/action`, so `/` ships none of that code; `landing.spec.ts` pins this with a network-request assertion.
- Freshness budget: none applicable. Nothing on this page is time-sensitive data.

### Gaps
- Corrected in this task, 2026-09-28: the External cell for LANDING-07 and LANDING-10, the two elements that actually issue `POST /api/waitlist`, now names Lovable as the SPA host whose static hosting triggers `authedFetch`'s origin rewrite onto `solyra-api-staging` (`src/main.tsx:14-25`, its own comment: "the landing page's own waitlist POST needs that rewrite too"; `src/lib/apiTargets.ts`), the same pattern SHARED-03 already uses. This replaces the previous version of this bullet, which argued no row's External cell had anything truthful to hold: that is still true for the eleven rows that make no request at all (their External cell stays `none`), but not for the two that call the network.
- LANDING-12 (the waitlist form's submitting/loading presentation) genuinely exists in `WaitlistSection.tsx`: the submit button is disabled and its text changes to "Joining…" while `status === 'submitting'` (`:19-23,77-79`). It has zero test coverage: neither a Vitest render test nor a solyra [tests/landing/landing.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/landing/landing.spec.ts) assertion exercises it. The one Playwright test that submits the form (`waitlist form rejects an invalid email with a visible error`) fills an invalid email, which fails client-side `validateEmail` and returns before `status` ever becomes `'submitting'`. UI-SCREENS.md's States table previously marked both LANDING-12 and LANDING-13 "absent" in source; reading `WaitlistSection.tsx` directly shows both presentations exist, so both cells are corrected in this task. The real, code-verified gap is test coverage, not presence: Te is unticked for LANDING-12 only, since LANDING-13 (the error state) is exercised by `waitlist.test.ts`, `tests/api/test_waitlist_router.py` and `landing.spec.ts` together.
- LANDING-11 ("See a live day", `Hero.tsx:41`, scrolling to `DailyRhythm`'s `id="learn"`) has no test in either repo, confirmed by searching `src/` and `tests/` for `#learn` and "See a live day"; its Chain Tests cell is `none` with this reason rather than left blank.
- [test_waitlist_router.py](../../tests/api/test_waitlist_router.py), cited by LANDING-10 and LANDING-13, fails at collection when run as a single file: its import (`tests/api/test_waitlist_router.py:32`) reaches `platform/api/routers/waitlist.py:24`'s `from api.schemas import (...)`, which needs `platform/` on `sys.path`, added elsewhere in a full `pytest tests/api` run but not by this file alone. A reproduction on this branch gave `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'api'` (filed as [stocks#1226](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1226)).

### Backend notes
- Auth is global ASGI middleware, not a per-router dependency. It verifies a bearer token only when `AUTH_MODE=firebase`; in `iap` and `open` it calls through (see [09-SECURITY-AUTH.md](09-SECURITY-AUTH.md)).
- The open prefixes are `/api/health`, `/api/me`, `/api/config/firebase`, `/api/waitlist` in [auth.py:69](../../platform/api/auth.py#L69) and must stay in sync with `OPEN_PREFIXES` in solyra [src/lib/authedFetch.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/authedFetch.ts) (area 00, SHARED-02).
- Boot order (client-side, no backend job feeds this area): `ConfigGate` fetches `/api/config/firebase` once per page load (module-level `bootPromise`), then in `firebase` mode dynamically imports and awaits the Firebase facade before `AuthGate` reads any auth state. A logged-out visit to `/` never pays this cost (issue #26).
- Freshness budget: none, identity and config are read fresh on every boot; `useUser`'s `/api/me` query has a 30-second `staleTime` so a role granted or revoked mid-session converges on the next mount/focus refetch, not instantly.

### Gaps
- The design's backend diagram labels the dashboard "no per-user auth" and the platform service "FastAPI + React"; both predate the #957 split (see SHARED, area 00).
- AUTH-03, AUTH-04 and AUTH-09 stay unticked at Te: the only Playwright test that genuinely asserts each row's own behavior, the framed new-tab variant for AUTH-03 (its not-framed branch is covered by a pre-existing test, but the row's own title names the framed variant), the inline sign-in error for AUTH-04, and the sign-out action for AUTH-09, is one this branch added to solyra [tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts) (`framed preview: the Google button opens a new tab instead of a popup`, `email sign-in shows the inline error when the identity call fails`, `sign out returns to the sign-in screen`). The e2e run this task cites ran on commit eca7078, this branch's base, so it predates all three tests; all three rows wait for a CI run on a head that includes them.
- AUTH-02 (the loading spinner) has no test of any kind, in either repo: every hermetic mock in solyra [tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts) fulfills the config and identity fetches synchronously, so the state is never observably in flight.

### Backend notes
- SHELL-04, 07, 09, 12-16 name the same handful of endpoints repeatedly because AppShell is a thin composition of independently-fetching widgets (`AuthStatusIndicator`/`useUser`, `MarketSessionBadge`/`useLiveStatus`, `MostActiveBar`/`useMostActive`) rather than one shell-level fetch; each widget owns its own loading/empty/error branch in source, not a shared shell-level state machine.
- `MostActiveBar` mounts once in `AppShell.tsx` (not per-page) on `/live`, `/charts`, `/options`, `/signals`, `/journal` only (`MOST_ACTIVE_BAR_ROUTES`), so it persists across navigation within that group instead of unmounting/remounting per route.
- Freshness budget: `useMostActive` has a 10-minute `staleTime` and a 15-minute `refetchInterval`; `useLiveStatus` refetches every 60s with a 30s `staleTime`; `useUser`'s `/api/me` query has a 30s `staleTime`. None of these are pipeline-fed on a cron the way Dashboard is; they are live/near-live reads, not daily-batch outputs.
- `fetch-top-movers` writes two different tables depending on its mode, verified by reading `gcp/fetchers/fetch_top_movers.py`'s `main()` in full. `top-movers-daily` (`15 16 * * 1-5`, 16:15 ET Mon-Fri, no flag) writes `top_movers_daily`, a table `/api/market/most-active` never reads. Only the `--intraday-snapshot` runs write `top_movers_intraday`, the table the handler actually queries: `top-movers-intraday-hourly` at `30 9-15 * * 1-5` (09:30-15:30 ET Mon-Fri) and `top-movers-intraday-close` at `5 16 * * 1-5` (16:05 ET Mon-Fri), both declared via `_schedule_with_args` in [gcp/deploy.sh](../../gcp/deploy.sh). The Data column above now cites only these two schedules; see Gaps for what it cited before this task.

### Gaps
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` markers were found in `platform/api/routers/live.py` or `platform/api/main.py`'s most-active/health handlers.
- SHELL-05, SHELL-12, SHELL-13, SHELL-14's Data cell previously cited `market_data_intraday` and the `top-movers-daily` schedule; neither is read by `market_most_active` (verified by reading `platform/api/main.py:1510-1584` in full, which queries only `top_movers_intraday`) and `top-movers-daily` does not write to the table the handler reads (verified by reading `gcp/fetchers/fetch_top_movers.py`'s `main()`, which reaches `top_movers_intraday` only via the `--intraday-snapshot` branch). Corrected in this task's T-gate commit.
- SHELL-02's Tests cell previously cited `navigation.spec.ts`'s `auth status lives at the menu bottom, not the bar`, which drives `TopTabs`'s mobile hamburger menu at the default `top-tabs` nav pattern and never mounts `Header` (sidebar-mode only). SHELL-11's Tests cell previously cited `navigation.spec.ts` generically, but every test in that file runs in open auth mode (`MOCK_FIREBASE_CONFIG_OPEN`), in which `SignOutButton` always returns `null`; no test there can render it. Both corrected in this task's T-gate commit, citing this task's new theme-toggle test (SHELL-02) and Task 13's `auth-gate.spec.ts` sign-out test (SHELL-11).
- SHELL-16's API/Backend cells previously cited `GET /api/me`, but that endpoint is open (`_OPEN_API_EXACT`, never 401s) and its values are not read by `AuthStatusBanner`; the actual `blocked` trigger is any gated `/api/*` 401 via `authedFetch.ts`'s `track()` helper, the same mechanism AUTH-08 documents. Corrected in this task's T-gate commit.
- No colocated or Playwright test was found for `RouteErrorBoundary.tsx` (SHELL-06); its Tests cell now reads `none (no test found)` rather than blank. `ReplayControl.tsx` (SHELL-09) has `popover-fit.spec.ts`'s `replay picker stays on screen`, but that test only asserts the open popover's viewport fit; no test exercises the picking/applying/clearing flow itself. `CommandPalette.tsx` (SHELL-08) gained its first test in this task. The `AuthStatusBanner`/`EmailVerificationBanner` pair (SHELL-04) has real backend pytest coverage of the `/api/me` identity read it partly depends on, but no test anywhere (Vitest or Playwright) targets either component's own render logic.
- SHELL-02, SHELL-08 and SHELL-11 stay unticked at Te for the same reason as AUTH-04 and AUTH-09 in area 02: the only Playwright test that genuinely asserts each row's own behavior, the Header/TopTabs theme toggle (SHELL-02), the command palette (SHELL-08), the sign-out action (SHELL-11), is one this branch added to solyra [tests/shared/navigation.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/navigation.spec.ts) or [tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/auth-gate.spec.ts). The e2e run this task cites ran on commit eca7078, this branch's base, so it predates all three tests; each row waits for a CI run on a head that includes them.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (all times America/New_York): `fetch-market-data` 23:00 Mon-Fri (`market_data_daily`, `market_data_intraday`) → `strat-engine` 23:35 Mon-Fri via `strat-engine-daily` (`strat_features_5m`, `strat_features_15m`) → `strat-engine` 02:00 Tue-Sat via `strat-enrich-daily` (`strat_features_levels_5m`, `strat_features_levels_15m`) → `backfill-daily-indicators` 02:30 Mon-Sat (heals NULL derived-indicator columns on `market_data_daily`; a second raw-scheduler entry, `backfill-indicators-weekly`, re-dispatches the same job Sundays at 03:00 ET with a `BACKFILL_MODE=full` override) → `phase6-playbook` 04:30 Mon-Fri (`playbook_cards`) → `fetch-news-sentiment-earnings` 06:00 Mon-Fri (`news_sentiment`) → `fetch-insider-transactions` / `fetch-economic-events` 07:00 Mon-Fri → `fetch-sec-filings` 07:00, 10:00, 13:00 and 17:00 Mon-Fri (`sec_filings`) → `fetch-news-sentiment` hourly 08:00-17:00 and `fetch-news-sentiment-topics` hourly 08:05-17:05 Mon-Fri (`news_sentiment`, these two and `fetch-sec-filings` through `if _schedule_verified` entries) → `auto-refresh-top-n` 08:10 Mon-Fri (enqueues `insight-pipeline` runs) → `fetch-premarket-refresh` 08:20 Mon-Fri (writes only `gap_pct`/`pre_*` on `market_data_daily`, inserting today's row without OHLC when it is absent) → `premarket-brief` 08:30 Mon-Fri (`premarket_analysis`) → `insight-pipeline` 08:45 Mon-Fri (`insight_reports`) → `fetch-av-options-realtime` every 5 min from 09:00 to 15:55 ET Mon-Fri (`etf_options_snapshots`, REALTIME) → `magnitude-inference` 09:25 Mon-Fri (`magnitude_per_bar_predictions`) → `fetch-earnings-calendar` 19:00 Mon-Fri → `fetch-earnings-history` 19:15 Mon-Fri (`market_data_daily` bars through `_run_backfill`) → `fetch-av-options-backfill` 21:00 Mon-Fri (`etf_options_snapshots`, EOD) and `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` 21:00 Mon-Sat and on the 1st (`market_data_intraday`) → `premarket-playbook-resolver` 21:15 Mon-Fri (`premarket_analysis` outcome columns) → `historical-signals-watchlist` 01:00 Tue-Sat (`historical_signals`, next session). Ordering for `market_data_daily` is enforced by the schedule itself (08:20 refresh runs before the 08:30 brief; the 23:00 nightly fetch is canonical and runs after close); see [05-c §4](infrastructure/05-c-DATA_DEPENDENCIES.md#4-multi-writer-tables-coordination-risks).
- `premarket_analysis` is a 3-writer table (`premarket-brief`, `premarket-playbook-resolver` at 21:15 Mon-Fri, `signal-monitor` at 09:25 Mon-Fri); the brief handler's `WHERE run_kind = 'live'` filter picks among them at read time, and which job produced the row read by DASHBOARD-01 on any given request was not traced further than that filter.
- Freshness the page assumes: the brief bullets read as "today's brief", which per the pipeline order above is not present until 08:30 ET on a trading day; before that the briefing strip shows the `Unavailable` fallback rather than yesterday's data relabeled as today's. `useInsightReport`, `useFetch` (brief/playbook/signals/sectors) all carry a 60s `staleTime`; the playbook query additionally re-polls every 15 minutes in live mode so the server's own `max_age_days` 503 surfaces within that window.
- Review mode (DASHBOARD-15) does not introduce new endpoints of its own: `reviewDate`/`reviewTime` add a `?date=`/`end_date=`/`end_time=` query parameter onto the brief, playbook and signals requests already listed under DASHBOARD-01/02/05, and `useReviewQuote` additionally calls the same `/api/market/data/{ticker}/{date}` (1-minute bars) and `/api/market/reference/{ticker}/{date}` endpoints already listed under DASHBOARD-04/03 to reconstruct a synthetic as-of quote; see [useReviewQuote.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useReviewQuote.ts).
- DASHBOARD-21's `assemble_movement_statement` unconditionally assembles four pieces on every call (`lib/movement_statement.py:978-995`): continuation (the GCS-served strat-type model, via `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_serve.py`), levels/reach-rates (`premarket_analysis`, already listed), expected-move (`magnitude_per_bar_predictions` + `strat_features_{tf}`), and gamma regime (`etf_options_snapshots`, via `lib/agents/summarizers.py:summarize_gamma_levels`'s REALTIME-then-EOD tiered fallback). The continuation piece's `predict_one` (`gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_serve.py:368-375`) also reads Cloud SQL directly, via `load_strat_features_with_levels` (`gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_dataset.py`, called with `include_levels=True`), which LEFT JOINs `strat_features_levels_{tf}` (`strat_features_levels_5m`/`strat_features_levels_15m`, both runtime-created) onto `strat_features_{tf}`. The `strat-engine` Cloud Run job serves four roles in DASHBOARD-21's chain: its scheduled `strat-engine-daily` trigger (23:35 ET Mon-Fri) runs `strat_data_builder` to write `strat_features_5m`/`strat_features_15m`; its separate `strat-enrich-daily` trigger (02:00 ET Tue-Sat, a raw `gcloud scheduler jobs create http` entry in `gcp/deploy.sh`, not a `_schedule` helper call) re-dispatches the same job with a container-override `args` body to run `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_enrich_levels.py --mode=backfill-all`, writing the two levels tables; a manual dispatch runs a *different* module in the same image, `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_train.py`, which writes the per-cell GCS files the continuation piece also reads (`model.pkl`, `features.txt`, `classes.txt`, and `metrics_<epoch>.json` or `runs/<run_id>/metrics.json`, per its docstring and its uploads at lines 300-323); and an on-demand dispatch can run `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_ece_snapshot.py`, which writes the prefix-root `structure_brief_latest.json` that `predict_one` reads for its live-ECE mute (`gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_serve.py:241-249`, `:343`) and has no scheduler trigger (its docstring, lines 14-28, also documents a local run). Neither scheduled trigger retrains the model or writes that snapshot. The Data cell lists, per table, the jobs that write the columns this element reads. From `premarket_analysis` those are `price`, the eight slot prices, their `*_hit_ts` and `outcome_resolved_at` (`lib/movement_statement.py:286-340`, `:407-416`): `premarket-brief` writes the row, and `premarket-playbook-resolver` sets the hit timestamps and `outcome_resolved_at` (`gcp/premarket_playbook_resolver.py:453-475`) and fills the slot prices only when both trigger prices are NULL (`gcp/premarket_playbook_resolver.py:359-374`, `:478-494`). The table's third writer in [05-c §4](infrastructure/05-c-DATA_DEPENDENCIES.md#4-multi-writer-tables-coordination-risks), `signal-monitor`, updates only `puts_reanchor_*` (`gcp/signal_monitor.py:553-558`), which neither query selects. From `market_data_daily` the level map uses each row's date, OHLC and `atr_14` (`platform/api/routers/dashboard.py:457-518`), so `backfill-daily-indicators` is listed for `atr_14` and `fetch-premarket-refresh` is not: it writes only `pre_*` and `gap_pct` (`gcp/fetchers/fetch_premarket_refresh.py:251-260`), which `build_level_map` does not use (`lib/strat_levels.py:1234-1236`), and the row it inserts for today has no OHLC and is dropped (`platform/api/routers/dashboard.py:457-458`, `:471`) (the levels join's silent fallback in this same chain is filed as [stocks#1218](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1218)).

### Gaps
- **04-BACKEND-API.md says `/api/market/data/{ticker}/{date}` touches `market_data_daily`; the handler reads `market_data_intraday`.** Verified by reading `_load_date_data` ([main.py:1595-1624](../../platform/api/main.py#L1595)): every branch is `SELECT ... FROM market_data_intraday`. This endpoint feeds DASHBOARD-04 and DASHBOARD-15; the Data cells above record what the code does.
- **04-BACKEND-API.md's Tables-touched for `/api/catalysts/events` omits `news_sentiment`.** The handler queries it directly ([catalysts.py:114](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L114)) and stamps the `"AV news"` source tag ([catalysts.py:405](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L405)) that DASHBOARD-09's News card filters on; the same endpoint genuinely serves both the Catalysts list and the News card from one fetch. Recorded on DASHBOARD-06 and DASHBOARD-09.
- **Benzinga is not in DASHBOARD-06's or DASHBOARD-09's path.** The page asks for the events with `date_from` and `date_to` only ([DashboardPage.tsx:355](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/DashboardPage.tsx#L355)), and the handler calls Benzinga only with `BENZINGA_API_KEY` on the service ([catalysts.py:136-140](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L136)), which neither service sets (read 2026-09-28). Both cards therefore show what the five Cloud SQL reads return, `sec_filings` among them, a table 04-BACKEND-API.md also leaves out of the route's Tables touched.
- **04-BACKEND-API.md says `/api/signals/{ticker}` reads data "via lib/"; the handler issues its own inline SQL against `historical_signals`** (`_query_signals_sql`, [signals.py:180-212](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L180)). DASHBOARD-05's Data cell names `historical_signals` directly rather than repeating "via lib/".
- **05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md's job table lists `phase6-playbook`'s schedule as "manual"; `gcp/deploy.sh` has a live, uncommented scheduler entry** `_schedule "phase6-playbook-daily" "30 4 * * 1-5" "phase6-playbook"` ([gcp/deploy.sh:4504](../../gcp/deploy.sh)). DASHBOARD-02's Data cell uses the schedule the code declares.
- The page renders a fourth live card, `MovementRead` ([src/components/dashboard/MovementRead.tsx](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/dashboard/MovementRead.tsx)), gated behind `MOVEMENT_STATEMENT_ENABLED` and hidden in review mode. This was flagged here as missing a Checklist row in the first draft of this task; the controller ruled to append it as DASHBOARD-21 (IDs never renumber) rather than leave it uncovered, so it now has both a Checklist row and a Chain row above.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` markers were found in `platform/api/routers/dashboard.py`, `platform/api/main.py`'s market/sectors/most-active handlers, `platform/api/routers/signals.py`, `platform/api/routers/catalysts.py`, `platform/api/routers/playbook.py` or `platform/api/routers/insights.py`'s report handler.
- **The brief handler swallows read failures and defaults missing streaks to 0.** `GET /api/dashboard/brief/{ticker}` wraps its `premarket_analysis` and `market_data_daily` reads each in a bare `except Exception`, logs a warning and continues with whatever it already has, and `consecutive_up`/`consecutive_down` default to `0` when missing, in two places (`platform/api/routers/dashboard.py:130`, `:139-140`, `:197`, `:202-203`, `:223-224`), the ambiguous-zero shape Rule 4 forbids for a streaks field (filed as [stocks#1222](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1222)).
- DASHBOARD-21's Tests cell cites [test_movement_statement_router.py](../../tests/api/test_movement_statement_router.py)'s nan-close test, `test_endpoint_nan_close_does_not_500_and_carries_no_nan`, which fails inside `pytest tests/api` because its patch target (`tests/api/test_movement_statement_router.py:550`) resolves the `strat_pred_serve` submodule off `gcp.research.strat_engine` by attribute lookup, which only succeeds when an earlier test in the run already imported it; run alone the file instead skips at module level (`tests/api/test_movement_statement_router.py:39`). A reproduction on this branch gave `AttributeError: module 'gcp.research.strat_engine' has no attribute 'strat_pred_serve'` (filed as [stocks#1225](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1225)).

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York, from the trigger crons in [gcp/deploy.sh](../../gcp/deploy.sh)): `fetch-earnings-history` 19:15 Mon-Fri and Sun (`market_data_daily`, through its chained `_run_backfill`) → `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` 21:00 Mon-Sat via `av-intraday-nightly` and 21:00 on the 1st via `av-intraday-monthly` (`market_data_intraday`) → `fetch-market-data` 23:00 Mon-Fri (`market_data_intraday`, `market_data_daily`). The live path waits on none of them: quote and history are AlphaVantage calls made per request, and only avg-volume and the review-mode reads touch Cloud SQL.
- Freshness the page assumes, from its hooks: quote every 15 s (`staleTime` 10 s) and history every 60 s (30 s), both only while `livePolling` · session status every 60 s (30 s) · avg-volume `staleTime` one hour · indicators `staleTime` 10 s, keyed on the bar count, last bar time, price, volume and average volume · the review-mode day bars one hour ([LiveMarketPage.tsx:35-47](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/LiveMarketPage.tsx#L35)). The live router keeps no server-side cache.
- Writers left out of the `market_data_daily` cells, with the lines that exclude them: `backfill-daily-indicators` writes only indicator and Strat columns (`gcp/fetchers/backfill_daily_indicators.py:350-351`, mapped by `gcp/database.py:1044`), and of those the reference handler's `week` block reads only `rsi_14`, which this page does not render · `fetch-premarket-refresh` inserts only `ticker`, `date`, `pre_*` and `gap_pct` (`gcp/fetchers/fetch_premarket_refresh.py:251-252`), a row dated today with no OHLC or `volume` (avg-volume filters `volume IS NOT NULL`, `platform/api/routers/live.py:409`) · `premarket-brief` only deletes rows `WHERE ticker = :t AND close IS NULL` (`gcp/premarket_brief.py:257-258`).
- Every writer of `market_data_intraday` stores whole bars, in one of two stored conventions (CLAUDE.md §3.9). The market-data handler reads both through `stored_intraday_to_eastern` (`lib/eastern_time.py:194`, aliased at `platform/api/main.py:1592`), and dates from `TRUE_UTC_FROM` (`lib/eastern_time.py:191`, 2026-09-28) are true UTC by construction.
- Producer secrets and tests. The scheduled producers take the shared `${DB_SECRET_FLAG}` (how 05 counts it is recorded in SHARED's Gaps), and `intraday-bulk-backfill` sets its own `--set-secrets` (`gcp/deploy.sh:3744-3750`). Producer tests: `fetch-market-data` [test_fetch_market_data_backfill_sleep.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_market_data_backfill_sleep.py), [test_fetch_market_data_fail_fast.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_market_data_fail_fast.py), [test_fetch_market_data_intraday_optional.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_market_data_intraday_optional.py), [test_fetch_market_data_universe.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_market_data_universe.py), [test_fetch_market_data_vix.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_market_data_vix.py) · all intraday writers [test_intraday_writers_utc.py](../../tests/gcp/test_intraday_writers_utc.py) · `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` and `intraday-bulk-backfill` [test_intraday_bulk_failure_classification.py](../../tests/gcp/test_intraday_bulk_failure_classification.py), [test_intraday_replace_months.py](../../tests/gcp/test_intraday_replace_months.py) · `fetch-earnings-history` [test_fetch_earnings_history.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_earnings_history.py) · `backfill-ticker` [test_backfill_ticker.py](../../tests/gcp/test_backfill_ticker.py).
- The request contract of every API-served row in areas 05 to 08 is also checked by solyra [contract.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/contract.test.ts): each request's verb and path against the vendored snapshot, the query parameter names it sends, sample request bodies (indicators, signal-series, greeks, watchlist add, journal POST and PATCH, replay-trades) and the mock payloads. It passed 14 of 14 on 2026-09-28 and is not repeated per row.

### Gaps
- [#928](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/928) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md. It concerns override resolution in `lib/signals.py` (`_latest_overrides`, audit finding C-04). The setup cards do not reach that code: they come from `_build_signals` inside the router ([live.py:680](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L680)), and the indicators handler never passes a ticker to `lib/signals.py`. No row on this page leads to the issue's code path.
- **An indicators failure renders as a computed result.** Nothing reads `indicatorsQuery.isError` ([LiveMarketPage.tsx:218-219](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/LiveMarketPage.tsx#L218) substitutes `EMPTY_INDICATORS` and `EMPTY_SIGNALS`). A hermetic render on 2026-09-28 with bars and a quote in hand and a failed `POST /api/live/indicators` showed every tile as `--` and both setup cards as `0/0 met 0%`, with no error text anywhere on the page. A `0%` strength is a value the reader cannot tell from a measured one (Rule 4) (filed as [solyra#75](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/75)).
- **Every quote failure is reported as a key or rate-limit problem.** `quoteError` is the query's `isError` for any non-OK status ([useLiveQuote.ts:26-27](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useLiveQuote.ts#L26)). The same render with a 401 on the quote showed `Live data unavailable, API key not configured or rate limited.` and no sign-in prompt: the page has no `SignInBanner`, and `DataGate` replaces the body only when the user is signed out (LIVE-13).
- **avg-volume falls back to AlphaVantage silently.** On a Cloud SQL failure the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` returns an empty frame and the handler answers from TIME_SERIES_DAILY ([live.py:404-428](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L404)). A hermetic probe with the connection forced to fail got HTTP 200 with `source: alphavantage` and one TIME_SERIES_DAILY call. The page never reads `source` (CLAUDE.md §3.7.1). The request also carries no date and the query no date bound ([live.py:407-411](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L407)), so review mode's RVOL divides the review day's volume by the latest 20 sessions.
- **Market data serves the legacy parquet with no marker.** `_load_date_data` falls back to the GCS `raw/data/<ticker>/` parquets when Cloud SQL fails or returns no rows ([main.py:1668-1696](../../platform/api/main.py#L1668)), and the response carries no `source` field. A hermetic probe with the connection forced to fail got HTTP 200 from `gs://adept-mountain-474619-d4-trading-data/raw/data/iwm/minute/iwm_minute_20260220.parquet`, and a missing volume came back as `0` ([main.py:1004](../../platform/api/main.py#L1004)). The page then applies its own `?? 0` to volume in review mode ([LiveMarketPage.tsx:185](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/LiveMarketPage.tsx#L185), no AUDIT marker). The only writer of those parquets is the one-shot `gcp/migrate_to_gcp.py` upload (`upload_raw_parquets`, line 79). 04's table for this route is already recorded in Dashboard's Gaps (area 04).
- **`MARKET_HOLIDAYS_2026` is the only holiday source** ([live.py:109-120](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L109)). Calling `_is_market_open` on 2026-09-28 returned `(False, 'closed')` for 2026-12-25 10:00 ET and `(True, 'regular')` for 2027-01-01 and 2027-01-18 10:00 ET, so from January 2027 the session bar reports holidays as regular sessions.
- `useReferenceLevels` returns `null` for any non-OK response ([useMarketData.ts:87](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useMarketData.ts#L87)), a `return null` in a data-access hook that Rule 4 forbids. In review mode that leaves the synthetic quote's change `null` (the case [reviewQuote.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/reviewQuote.test.ts) covers), with nothing saying the prior close failed to load.
- `GET /api/live/history/{ticker}` drops any bar that fails to parse ([live.py:372](../../platform/api/routers/live.py#L372), `except (KeyError, ValueError): continue`) without counting the drop in the response.
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for `/api/live/status`, `/api/live/quote/{ticker}` and `/api/live/history/{ticker}`, which read the clock or call AlphaVantage themselves. UI-SCREENS.md lists only `/api/market/data/` as this page's API calls and `load` as an absent state, and the design seed's issue list says `No loading state`. The page has two loading states (LIVE-09) and calls every route in the rows above through its hooks. 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md gives `backfill-ticker`, a writer of both price tables, one retry, and `deploy_backfill_ticker` sets `--max-retries 0` (`gcp/deploy.sh:1298`).
- The page cannot switch ticker itself (LIVE-07): the command palette's ticker entries navigate to `/charts`, and `TickerCombobox` is mounted on Dashboard, Options Flow, Signals, Insights and Journal only.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in the handlers this page reads (`platform/api/routers/live.py`, `platform/api/main.py`) or in the `lib/` modules they call, and every route in the rows above declares a `response_model`. The page's own unmarked `?? 0` is recorded above.
- No test was found for LIVE-05 (polling toggle), LIVE-06 (sound), LIVE-09, LIVE-10 or LIVE-12. LIVE-11's tests cover the handler only, not the banner.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `fetch-fred-rates` 06:30 daily (`daily_rates`) → `fetch-av-options-realtime` every 5 min from 09:00 to 15:55 Mon-Fri (`etf_options_snapshots`, REALTIME, SPY IWM QQQ) → `fetch-earnings-history` 19:15 Mon-Fri and Sun (`market_data_daily`) → `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` 21:00 Mon-Sat and `fetch-av-options-backfill` 21:00 Mon-Fri (`market_data_intraday`, `etf_options_snapshots` EOD) → `fetch-market-data` 23:00 Mon-Fri (`market_data_intraday`, `market_data_daily`) → `historical-signals-watchlist` 01:00 Tue-Sat (`historical_signals`, for the previous session) → `etf-options-retention` 02:00 daily. On the 1st, `fetch-av-options-backfill` also runs at 05:00 and `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` at 21:00. Nothing scheduled produces the backtester's CSVs (Gaps).
- Freshness the page assumes: `useMarketData` and `useReferenceLevels` never go stale (`staleTime: Infinity`) · the dates list five minutes · journal trades 10 s · indicators and signal-series 10 s · similar setups 60 s · the backtester 30 s · gamma levels one hour with `retry: false`. Server-side, the dates list is cached one hour behind a `MAX(ts)` probe (`platform/api/main.py:578-582`), backtest results and equity one hour and the run list ten minutes (`platform/api/routers/backtest.py:116-118`), and the chain behind `/levels` twelve hours (`platform/api/routers/options.py:90`).
- Three voters feed three elements, and they are different computations ([#912](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/912)): the Sig overlay and the similar card's trigger use the mean-reversion voter in `lib/signals.py` (`generate_signals`, called without a ticker, so no override read), the Strategy conditions card uses `lib/chart_voter.py`, and the `historical_signals` rows the similar card counts come from the momentum voter in `lib/trading_analysis.py`, the `--strategy` default at `scripts/run_historical_signals.py:105`.
- The scorecard handler reads more than 04 lists. Bars come through `_load_date_data` (`platform/api/routers/backtest.py:482-483`, the same Cloud SQL then GCS path as market data), overrides through `lib/backtest.py:848-850` into `lib/strategies/exit_config_overrides.py`, whose exit-target getters fall back to Tier-B defaults (lines 155-167), and configuration through `load_config` (`lib/config.py:704`), which reads `alert_config.json` from the working directory and returns dataclass defaults when it is absent. `platform/Dockerfile` copies only `lib/`, `gcp/`, `scripts/` and `platform/api/` (lines 40-45), so the service should be running the benchmark on those defaults, while the jobs image does copy the file (`gcp/Dockerfile:23`). Not checked against a running container.
- `PATCH /api/journal/trades/{trade_id}` is wired on this page (`useCloseChartTrade` behind `onTradeExited`), but nothing here triggers it: `startExitMode` is called only from [JournalPage.tsx:627](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L627), as the comment at [ChartsPage.tsx:218-221](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L218) says. No row owns it.
- Writers left out, with the lines that exclude them: `scripts/backfill_journal_embeddings.py` updates only `journal_entries.embedding` (lines 79-80), which neither the GET (`platform/api/routers/journal.py:892-901`) nor the replay query selects · `scripts/backfill_timeframe_tags.py` updates only `timeframe_tag` and `expected_hold_min` on `historical_signals` (lines 172-174) · `compute-spx-greeks-backfill` updates only the `*_computed` columns of `etf_options_snapshots` (`scripts/maintenance/compute_spx_greeks.py:149-155`), which the chain query behind `/levels` does not select (`platform/api/routers/options.py:562-579`). The `market_data_daily` exclusions are the ones in Live's Backend notes.
- Producer tests beyond Live's: `historical-signals-watchlist` [test_historical_signals.py](../../tests/gcp/test_historical_signals.py), [test_run_historical_signals_args.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_args.py), [test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py), [test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py) · options fetchers [test_fetch_av_historical_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_historical_options.py), [test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py) · `etf-options-retention` [test_options_retention_job.py](../../tests/gcp/test_options_retention_job.py) · `param-sweep` [test_param_sweep_persistence.py](../../tests/integration/test_param_sweep_persistence.py) (integration, outside `make test`) · `fetch-fred-rates`: no test found.

### Gaps
- [#912](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/912) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md: duplicate indicator implementations behind one name. The similar card is where it shows. It asks for rows matching a mean-reversion fire's direction and score, and the query has no `strategy` predicate ([signals.py:354-356](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L354)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 (`scripts/db_query_cr.sh`, execution `db-query-l4hpx`) found every `historical_signals` row labelled `momentum`, with `signal_strength` 3 to 7, and strengths 3 and 4 ending on 2026-05-04. So the card reports the other voter's outcomes, and a score-3 or score-4 fire is matched only against rows from 2026-05-04 or earlier.
- **Nothing writes the backtester's objects.** The only code naming the prefix reads it (`platform/api/routers/backtest.py:102`). `scripts/run_backtest.py` writes CSVs to the local `data/backtest_results` directory (`lib/config.py:166`), and `upload_raw_parquets` (`gcp/migrate_to_gcp.py:79-91`) uploads `*.parquet` only. A read-only listing on 2026-09-28 found 111 objects (53.41 MiB). The 90 `backtest_*` and `equity_*` CSVs (IWM 17, QQQ 11, SPY 12, SPX 5 runs) were all uploaded between 2026-02-23T01:36:50Z and 01:36:54Z, and the newest object is from 2026-02-23T10:40:04Z. 04 gives `via lib/` for all three routes, which read GCS through `platform/api/gcs_reader.py` and call no `lib/` module.
- **A GCS outage reads as no results.** `list_matching_blobs` returns `[]` on any storage error (`platform/api/gcs_reader.py:75-95`), and a hermetic probe with the client forced to fail got a 404 `No backtest results found in GCS for ticker 'IWM'`. The page then shows its not-found advice for every error ([BacktesterSection.tsx:411-417](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/backtest/BacktesterSection.tsx#L411)). The response's `trades` rows are free-form (`list[dict[str, Any]]`, `platform/api/schemas.py:1140`), so the contract check validates them trivially.
- **The replay scorecard's failure mode contradicts its docstring.** The docstring promises a 500 on any Cloud SQL error ([backtest.py:516-518](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L516)), but `_replay_journal_query` forwards to the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` ([backtest.py:486-490](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L486)), and a hermetic probe with the connection forced to fail got 404 `no matching trades found`. `platform/api/routers/journal.py` moved its own `_journal_query` to the strict helper for this reason (`platform/api/routers/journal.py:116-122`). 04 lists only `journal_entries` for this route, which also reads `market_data_intraday` and `exit_config_overrides` (Backend notes).
- **The scorecard needs a trade that Charts cannot close.** Mark Entry posts no exit ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:390-415](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L390)), which creates an ACTIVE trade (`test_create_active_trade_without_exit_returns_null_return_pct` in [test_journal_phase2.py](../../tests/api/test_journal_phase2.py) passed on 2026-09-28), and nothing on this page closes one (Backend notes). Stop scores only closed session trades ([ChartsPage.tsx:238-252](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L238)), so from this page alone it ends with `Session ended: no closed trades to score`. The design seed's `Backtest my trades` button and journey no longer exist ([ChartsPage.tsx:226-229](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L226)).
- **The chart's own loading and error branches cannot render.** `WidgetState` shows its skeleton, `SignInEmptyState` or `WidgetError` before its children ([WidgetState.tsx](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/shared/WidgetState.tsx)), and a hermetic render on 2026-09-28 confirmed an erroring query never reaches the children. So `No market data available for this date` and `Markets may be closed` show only for a zero-bar day ([ChartsPage.tsx:765-770](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L765)), not for the error branch at [ChartsPage.tsx:722-733](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L722).
- The Strategy conditions card substitutes `EMPTY_CHART_VOTER` for a missing response, error included ([ChartsPage.tsx:393](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L393), `isError` is never read), while the comment at [ChartsPage.tsx:56-59](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L56) says the empty voter shows only before the query resolves.
- Silent fallbacks on this page's path: the `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker at [ChartsPage.tsx:364](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L364) (candle volume `?? 0` sent to indicators and signal-series) · `useReferenceLevels` returns `null` on any non-OK response ([useMarketData.ts:87](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useMarketData.ts#L87)), so a failed reference call just hides the Prev lines · market data's unmarked GCS fallback and `0` volume, recorded in Live's Gaps.
- The comment at [ChartsPage.tsx:801-803](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L801) says the similar card posts to `/api/live/similar-setups`. No such route exists in the snapshot: the card sends a GET to `/api/signals/{ticker}/similar` ([useSimilarSetups.ts:75](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useSimilarSetups.ts#L75)).
- `Gamma toggle is hidden for non-ETF tickers` asserts only that the toggle is visible for IWM, and says it passes vacuously ([gamma-levels.spec.ts:132-141](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/gamma-levels.spec.ts#L132)). The hidden case is untested.
- Sources that disagree with the code: the design seed feeds the conditions card from `POST /api/live/signal-series`, but the card renders the `chart_voter` slice of `POST /api/live/indicators` ([ChartsPage.tsx:780-786](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L780)). UI-SCREENS.md lists [charts-cards.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/charts-cards.spec.ts) and [ticker-combobox.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/dashboard/ticker-combobox.spec.ts) as the page's specs, while [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts) and [gamma-levels.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/gamma-levels.spec.ts) also drive it.
- Apart from solyra's marker at [ChartsPage.tsx:364](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L364) (above), no `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits on this page's path: none in `platform/api/main.py`, `platform/api/routers/journal.py`, `platform/api/routers/signals.py`, `platform/api/routers/backtest.py`, `platform/api/routers/live.py`, `platform/api/routers/options.py` or the `lib/` modules they call. Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`, and the backtest results' free-form `trades` rows are recorded above.
- No test was found for CHARTS-03, CHARTS-09's timeframe buttons or CHARTS-13. CHARTS-07's solyra coverage is formatter-only.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `fetch-fred-rates` 06:30 daily (`daily_rates`) → `fetch-av-options-realtime` every 5 min from 09:00 to 15:55 Mon-Fri (REALTIME rows, SPY IWM QQQ) → `fetch-av-options-backfill` 21:00 Mon-Fri (EOD rows for SPY IWM QQQ SPX, from the job's own args, not the trigger's) → `etf-options-retention` 02:00 daily (deletes REALTIME rows older than 30 days) · `fetch-av-options-backfill` again at 05:00 on the 1st. SPX has no realtime producer, so Trinity's SPX panel and any SPX view read EOD rows only.
- Off-list tickers take a different path. `/api/options/dates/{ticker}`, the chain, the live proxy and `/levels` accept SPY, IWM, QQQ and SPX only (`VALID_TICKERS`, `platform/api/routers/options.py:86`, 400 otherwise). The live grid instead fetches REALTIME_OPTIONS on demand for a ticker outside `GRID_SCHEDULED_TICKERS` when Cloud SQL has nothing, writes the rows back (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:594`) and caps each client IP at 10 distinct tickers per 60 s (lines 375-376, 408-436).
- Freshness the page assumes: the live grid refetches every 60 s (`staleTime` 50 s), the historical grid and `/levels` hold one hour, the dates lists five minutes (`STALE_TIME_MS`). Server-side, the live grid is cached 60 s, the historical grid and the chain twelve hours, the dates lists twelve hours behind a newest-date probe, and the live chain proxy five minutes (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:100-101`, `platform/api/routers/options.py:90-116`).
- `daily_rates` is reached two calls deep: `build_summary` and `build_grid_summary` call `get_rate_and_yield` (`lib/gamma.py:1086-1088`, `:1298-1300`), which reads the table strictly (`lib/options_greeks.py:195-205`). An unavailable rate leaves the gamma flip `None` rather than a computed value.
- Writers left out: `compute-spx-greeks-backfill` updates only the `*_computed` columns (`scripts/maintenance/compute_spx_greeks.py:149-155`), which neither the chain query (`platform/api/routers/options.py:562-579`) nor the grid loaders (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:236-254`) select.
- The page itself holds no loading, empty or error state: each view carries its own (OPTIONS-09 to 11). Producer tests: [test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_realtime_options.py), [test_fetch_av_historical_options.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_av_historical_options.py), [test_options_retention_job.py](../../tests/gcp/test_options_retention_job.py), and for the picker's watchlist write [test_watchlist_helper.py](../../tests/gcp/test_watchlist_helper.py). `fetch-fred-rates`: no test found.

### Gaps
- [#826](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/826) (open) is this page's blocking issue: `or 0` on gamma and open interest with no coverage gate. Gamma now has a gate in `build_summary` (`lib/gamma.py:1057`), but `open_interest or 0.0` remains in the aggregators (`lib/gamma.py:276`, `:348`, `:452-454`, `:702`) and `greeks_coverage` counts only gamma (`lib/gamma.py:203-229`). `POST /api/options/greeks` aggregates with no gate ([options.py:784](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L784)), and the grid's `AUDIT-2026-09-07` marker ([grid.py:550](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L550)) records that `build_grid_summary` has none either.
- **Swing Mode shows mock nodes beside real levels.** The comment says no mock value is shown next to a real grid ([SwingMode.tsx:811-813](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L811)), but with real `/levels` data the legend's Hedge chip reads `HS.nodes.hedge[0]` ([SwingMode.tsx:415](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L415)) and the node list adds the mock midpoints and hedges ([SwingMode.tsx:526-532](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L526)). A hermetic render on 2026-09-28 with a real IWM `/levels` payload showed `Hedge 580`, `591 Midpoint` and `580 Hedge` from [gammaMapMock.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/data/gammaMapMock.ts) next to the real King 222.
- **Failures render as empty data or as zeros.** Hermetic renders on 2026-09-28: a failed grid request shows `Data unavailable: No options grid available for this symbol.`, the text of a symbol with no grid ([SwingMode.tsx:616-622](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L616)) · a failed dates request shows `No gamma levels available for` on all three Trinity panels ([TrinityTab.tsx:57](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/TrinityTab.tsx#L57)) · a failed Greeks POST with a resolved spot shows `Total GEX +0 Positive` and `Put/Call OI 0.00 Bullish skew` from `EMPTY_GREEKS` ([ProfilesTab.tsx:326](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/ProfilesTab.tsx#L326), [useOptionsGreeks.ts:145-158](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useOptionsGreeks.ts#L145)), a fabricated flat reading (Rule 4) (filed as [solyra#74](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/74)).
- **Server failures read as no data.** Hermetic probes with the connection forced to fail: the chain answered 404 `No earlier data ingested for this ticker` ([options.py:580-604](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L580)), on which Profiles switches to the live AlphaVantage proxy, and the live grid answered 200 `unavailable` with the no-chain reason ([grid.py:229-285](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L229)). Both come from the swallowing `query_to_dataframe`. Profiles does show which source answered (`AlphaVantage Live` in the footer), not why.
- **The live grid calls any REALTIME snapshot from the last 20 days `realtime`.** `_load_chain_for_live` returns the newest REALTIME snapshot dated on or after `today - 20 days` with `days_behind` 0, and consults the EOD tiers only when there is none ([grid.py:232-263](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L232)). A hermetic probe for 2026-09-28 with only a 2026-09-10 REALTIME snapshot got `realtime`, `days_behind` 0 and a single query. Swing then shows the `LIVE` pill ([SwingMode.tsx:214](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L214)) and `live {sym} dealer exposure` in its banner, including on evenings and weekends, when the day's 21:00 EOD chain is newer than the last REALTIME snapshot (filed as [stocks#1211](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1211)).
- **Today's chain is pinned to its first snapshot.** The dates list includes days with only REALTIME rows, the chain takes that day's `MAX(snapshot_ts)`, and the result is cached per `(ticker, date)` for twelve hours (`platform/api/routers/options.py:89-90`, `:562-579`). A hermetic probe served a later 19:55Z snapshot's request with the cached 14:00Z chain (`cached: true`). So Profiles and Trinity, which default to the newest date, can show an intraday snapshot as the day's chain for up to twelve hours (filed as [stocks#1212](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1212)).
- The expiry chips (All, 0DTE, Weekly, Monthly, Quarterly) change only their own highlight: `expiryFilter` never reaches the grid query or `RealHeatmapGrid` ([SwingMode.tsx:794](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L794), [SwingMode.tsx:936-960](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L936)).
- `daily_rates.sp500_div_yld` is a configured constant, not a measurement: `fetch-fred-rates` writes `SP500_DIV_YIELD_DEFAULT = 0.013` into every row (`gcp/fetchers/fetch_fred_rates.py:62`, `:119`), and the gamma flip on `/levels` and the grid uses it.
- `search_tickers` returns `[]` when the key is missing or AlphaVantage fails (`lib/ticker_info.py:414-433`). A hermetic probe got HTTP 200 with `results: []`, so the picker shows no matches although [ticker-combobox.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/dashboard/ticker-combobox.spec.ts) requires `search failure renders an inline error, never an empty "no matches" lie`.
- Sources that disagree with the code: the Checklist and the design seed name the views Heatseeker and Flowseeker, the code names them Gamma Map and Flow ([OptionsFlowPage.tsx:24-28](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/OptionsFlowPage.tsx#L24)), and [options-flow.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/options/options-flow.spec.ts) asserts `the borrowed internal module names never appear in the app UI`. The seed's `Pick an expiration date` re-fetches the grid, levels and chain, but no view picks an expiration: Profiles steps through snapshot dates and Swing's chips are inert (above). The seed says Swing falls back to a mock grid, the code renders `Data unavailable` instead ([SwingMode.tsx:616-622](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L616)). The seed says the mock views await `/api/options/flow` and `/contract/{id}/tape`, and neither is in the snapshot. Stale comments still call Swing mock ([OptionsFlowPage.tsx:13](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/OptionsFlowPage.tsx#L13), [GammaMapSection.tsx:9-10](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/GammaMapSection.tsx#L9)).
- 04 and 05 against the code: 04 gives `via lib/` for both grid routes, which read `etf_options_snapshots` themselves ([grid.py:255](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L255), [grid.py:285](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L285), [grid.py:332](../../platform/api/routers/grid.py#L332)), reach `daily_rates` through `lib/` and write on demand, and for `/api/options/live/{ticker}/{date_str}`, which calls AlphaVantage itself ([options.py:665](../../platform/api/routers/options.py#L665)). 04 lists only `etf_options_snapshots` for `/levels`, which also reads `daily_rates`. 05's job table lists only `DB_PASS` for `fetch-av-options-backfill`, `fetch-av-options-realtime` and `intraday-bulk-backfill`, whose inline `--set-secrets` also carry `av-api-key`, and for `intraday-bulk-backfill` `gh-stocks-repo-pat` (`gcp/deploy.sh:2270-2272`, `:2318-2320`, `:3744-3750`).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits on this page's server path. The one in `lib/agents/summarizers.py` (line 285) is inside `summarize_market_context`, and the grid imports only `classify_gamma_freshness` from that module (`platform/api/routers/grid.py:78-81`). Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- UI-SCREENS.md lists no states for this page (`none detected`), since they live in the child views (OPTIONS-09 to 13). No test was found for OPTIONS-04 or OPTIONS-09 to 11, and no test exercises the expiry chips.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `signal-monitor` 09:25 Mon-Fri (polls AlphaVantage intraday bars through the session, logs each fire as a `trades` row with `run_kind = 'live'` through `gcp/trade_logger.py` and sets its exit, `gcp/signal_monitor.py:1774-1790`, `:2427-2436`) → `signal-monitor-eod-resolver` 16:30 Mon-Fri (closes trades left open, `gcp/signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py:416`) → `historical-signals-watchlist` 01:00 Tue-Sat (runs `scripts/run_historical_signals.py` with `--from-watchlist` over the active watchlist tickers, `gcp/deploy.sh:660-689`, and appends the previous session to `historical_signals`). The ORB triggers (`orb-15m-alert`, `orb-30m-alert`) run `signal-monitor` in `--mode=orb-snapshot`, which writes no `trades` row (`gcp/signal_monitor.py:2516`).
- Freshness the page assumes: the signals query and the performance summary both hold five minutes, and neither the handler nor the hook refreshes on a schedule. The newest `historical_signals` row is the previous session's, since the writer runs at 01:00 (a read-only query on 2026-09-28 found the latest IWM, SPY and QQQ rows on 2026-09-25).
- The picker in the header is the same component as OPTIONS-06, so a `new`-badged pick adds the ticker to `watchlists`, the table `historical-signals-watchlist` iterates. When Cloud SQL is not configured (local dev only), the handler reads the legacy GCS parquets instead (`platform/api/routers/signals.py:90`, `:253`, `source: gcs_parquet`), never as a fallback from a failed query.
- Writers left out: `scripts/backfill_timeframe_tags.py` updates only `timeframe_tag` and `expected_hold_min` (lines 172-174), neither of which the handler selects (`platform/api/routers/signals.py:190-212`).
- Producer tests: `historical-signals-watchlist` [test_historical_signals.py](../../tests/gcp/test_historical_signals.py), [test_run_historical_signals_args.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_args.py), [test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_provenance.py), [test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py](../../tests/scripts/test_run_historical_signals_strategy.py) · `signal-monitor` [test_signal_monitor_persist.py](../../tests/gcp/test_signal_monitor_persist.py), [test_trade_logger_reads.py](../../tests/gcp/test_trade_logger_reads.py) · `signal-monitor-eod-resolver` [test_signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py](../../tests/gcp/test_signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py) · the migrator's `trades` load [test_migrate_trades_tz_guard.py](../../tests/gcp/test_migrate_trades_tz_guard.py).

### Gaps
- [#905](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/905) closed as a duplicate of [#1206](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1206) on 2026-09-28, which is this page's blocking issue in its place, open but marked tracking only and not currently being worked: live alert scores do not rank outcomes. The Performance block shows exactly those live trades, the handler reads `trades` with `run_kind = 'live'` ([analytics.py:146-151](../../platform/api/routers/analytics.py#L146)), under the label `Performance · 90-day backtest` ([SignalsPage.tsx:211](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L211)). The hook's comments and the design seed also call them backtest aggregates ([useTradeAnalytics.ts:3-5](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useTradeAnalytics.ts#L3)).
- **The date filters see only the newest 5,000 rows.** The page asks for `limit=5000`, the handler returns the most recent rows ([signals.py:190-212](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L190)), and From and To filter in the browser ([SignalsPage.tsx:163-170](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L163)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-8zkw8`) found 189,980 IWM rows back to 2015-01-02, with the 5,000th newest at 2026-07-17. Outside review mode, an earlier range shows `No signals match your filters` while the header counts all 189,980 (filed as [stocks#1213](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1213)).
- **The Min score filter offers scores no row has.** The options run 5+ to 8+ ([SignalsPage.tsx:251-255](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L251)), and the same query as Charts' Gaps (`db-query-l4hpx`) found `signal_strength` 3 to 7 only, so 8+ always empties the table.
- **Review mode's cutoff is read as UTC.** `end_time` is documented as Eastern ([signals.py:228](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L228)), but the cutoff is cast in the database session ([signals.py:172-173](../../platform/api/routers/signals.py#L172)), which runs in UTC: a read-only query on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-ktktq`) returned `UTC` and cast `2026-09-25 10:30:00` to `10:30:00+00:00`. The same query found IWM rows since 2026-08-01 in UTC hours 4 to 7, which true UTC cannot hold (00:00 to 03:59 ET), and in hours 21 to 23 and 0, which Eastern labels cannot hold. `entry_time` therefore carries both conventions CLAUDE.md §3.9 describes for its source table, the cutoff is four hours early for the true-UTC rows (five under EST), and the Time column prints each row's stored string ([SignalsPage.tsx:72](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L72)) (filed as [stocks#1210](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1210)).
- **Every error reads as missing pipeline data.** Any failed request, a 503 outage and a 500 defect alike, renders `Signal data not found for {ticker}. Run the signals generation pipeline first.` (the `isError` branch at [SignalsPage.tsx:294-299](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L294)). A failed performance summary renders nothing: the block shows only when `closedTrades > 0` ([SignalsPage.tsx:209](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SignalsPage.tsx#L209)).
- 04 gives `via lib/` for `/api/signals/{ticker}`, and the handler's inline SQL is already recorded in Dashboard's Gaps (area 04).
- The design seed's backend map lists `historical_signals` among `signal-monitor`'s outputs, but `gcp/signal_monitor.py` never names the table, and its writer is `historical-signals-watchlist` (`gcp/historical_signals.py:121`, `:124`, `:235`). The seed's `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` entry gives only the monthly run and omits `av-intraday-nightly`. UI-SCREENS.md lists only `/api/signals/` among this page's calls, and the Performance block and the picker add four more (SIGNALS-01, SIGNALS-02).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/signals.py`, `platform/api/routers/analytics.py` or the picker's handlers, and every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- No test was found for SIGNALS-03, SIGNALS-05, SIGNALS-06, SIGNALS-08 or SIGNALS-11. SIGNALS-07's and SIGNALS-10's tests cover the handler only.
