### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York, from the trigger crons in [gcp/deploy.sh](../../gcp/deploy.sh)): `auto-refresh-top-n` 08:10 Mon-Fri (ranks the `default` owner's watchlist through `lib/agents/ranker/` and enqueues the top three tickers onto `insight-pipeline-queue`, each run recorded with trigger `on_demand`, `gcp/auto_refresh_top_n.py:219`, `:288`, `:294`) → `premarket-brief` 08:30 Mon-Fri (`premarket_analysis`, read by the House Views card) → `insight-pipeline` 08:45 Mon-Fri (the `default` owner's `in_insight` tickers, `gcp/insight_pipeline_job.py:772-773`, one execution per ticker through the same queue, trigger `scheduled`, writing `insight_runs`, `insight_reports` and `insight_reports_history`) → `insight-discord-push` 09:15 Mon-Fri, a separate job that posts the day's reports to Discord and is not on this page's path. The fetchers behind the report's inputs, with their times, are in INSIGHTS-07's Data cell.
- Freshness the page assumes, from its hooks: the report, the history and the brief hold for 60 s, a by-id report for five minutes, the ranking for five minutes, the admin roster for 30 s, and a run is polled every 3 s while `queued` or `running` with no limit ([useInsights.ts:181-185](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useInsights.ts#L181)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found the newest live reports for SPY, IWM and QQQ at 2026-09-25 12:46 UTC, 08:46 ET (execution `db-query-zdb25`), so until that day's run lands (08:10 for the tickers `auto-refresh-top-n` picks, 08:45 for the `in_insight` ones) the Briefing tab shows the previous session's report under its timestamp alone.
- The read path touches only `insight_reports`: the cards render what the pipeline assembled at generation time from the tables in INSIGHTS-07. `GET /api/insights/report/{ticker}` also feeds DASHBOARD-08 and `GET /api/dashboard/brief/{ticker}` DASHBOARD-01. The rows here follow the producers further than those cells: the `auto-refresh-top-n` runs, the queue's role in the fan-out, Vertex AI and `model_routing`.
- INSIGHTS-08's cutoff path uses every resource INSIGHTS-07 lists, so its cells restate them with the bound each read applies. A hermetic capture of the bundle's SQL on 2026-09-28 found the same six tables read live and with a 10:30 UTC cutoff (`earnings_calendar`, `economic_events`, `etf_options_snapshots`, `market_data_daily`, `news_sentiment`, `sec_filings`), and the reflection read, the planner's override read and the route snapshot take no cutoff argument.
- The header's `TickerCombobox` ([InsightsPage.tsx:117](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L117)) has no Checklist row. Its calls, `GET /api/insights/ticker/search`, `GET /api/market/coverage` and, for a `new`-badged pick, `POST /api/insights/watchlist/add`, are the same as OPTIONS-06 and SIGNALS-01.
- Producer tests: `insight-pipeline` [test_insight_pipeline_job.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_pipeline_job.py), [test_insight_pipeline_memory.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_pipeline_memory.py), [test_canonical_provenance.py](../../tests/gcp/test_canonical_provenance.py) · the fan-out and enqueue [test_insight_tasks.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_tasks.py) · `auto-refresh-top-n` [test_auto_refresh.py](../../tests/gcp/test_auto_refresh.py) · the pipeline's `lib/agents/` modules [test_agent_orchestrator.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_orchestrator.py), [test_agent_summarizers.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_summarizers.py), [test_agent_embeddings.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_embeddings.py), [test_agent_vertex_adapter.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_vertex_adapter.py), [test_agent_model_routing.py](../../tests/agents/test_agent_model_routing.py), [test_trade_planner.py](../../tests/lib/test_trade_planner.py), [test_ranker.py](../../tests/agents/test_ranker.py) · `insight-discord-push` [test_insight_discord_push.py](../../tests/gcp/test_insight_discord_push.py).
- The request contract of the API-served rows in areas 09 to 12 is also checked by solyra [contract.test.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/contract.test.ts): verbs and paths against the vendored snapshot, query parameter names, the mock payloads, and sample request bodies that include the chat, the watchlist add and the playbook evaluation. It passed 14 of 14 on 2026-09-28 and is not repeated per row.

### Gaps
- [#916](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/916) (open) is this page's blocking issue: ablate the agent graph and keep numeric levels to supplied structured fields. The headline entry, stop and targets come from `lib/agents/trade_planner.py` when persona plans compute, and from the LLM portfolio manager otherwise ([orchestrator.py:626-640](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L626)).
- A read-only query on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-8lfh7`) found an empty `persona_plans` on every live report through 2026-09-15 and on none from 2026-09-16, after PR #1102 (merged 2026-09-15) fixed the planner's silent failure. The History tab opens those older reports, whose headline plans are the LLM's, with no marker.
- **Refresh runs inside the API process on the deployed services.** Neither service sets `ENV` (read 2026-09-28), so `_is_local_dev()` is true ([insights.py:489-490](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L489)) and the run becomes a `BackgroundTask` recorded as `local_dev` ([insights.py:870-874](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L870)), never a queue task. A hermetic probe of the handler confirmed that branch, and a second with `ENV` set and `google.cloud.tasks_v2` unimportable took the same fallback (`gcp/insight_tasks.py:146-151`). A `pip install --dry-run --report` of the image's requirements resolved 92 packages without `google-cloud-tasks`, and the running image was not inspected. The services run 1 CPU and 2Gi with `run.googleapis.com/cpu-throttling=true` against the job's 4Gi, and whether a run finishes after the response is sent was not checked. `insight_runs` holds 10 `local_dev` rows, the newest from 2026-04-25 (execution `db-query-jq7t2`) (filed as [stocks#1214](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1214)).
- **Runs can stay `running` and the page polls them indefinitely.** Six `on_demand` runs (AMD three times, AVGO twice, MU once) sat in `running` from 2026-09-17 to 2026-09-25 (execution `db-query-wqqwr`). Two of them, AVGO and AMD on 2026-09-17, are the fan-out children the comment in `deploy_insight_pipeline` records as killed out of memory that day at the 4Gi limit, their retry standing down without re-running (`gcp/deploy.sh:584-588`), and the live job still runs 4Gi and 1 CPU (read 2026-09-28) against the function's 8Gi and 2 CPU. The other four were not checked. The page shows no failure either way: a failed request goes to the console ([InsightsPage.tsx:86-89](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L86)) and a `failed` run only stops the spinner ([InsightsPage.tsx:64-76](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L64)), its `error` never rendered.
- **A replay result is never shown as one.** A cutoff run writes `run_kind = 'replay'` ([insights.py:455](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L455)), the Briefing tab reads live rows only ([insights.py:228](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L228), [insights.py:240](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L240)), History lists every kind without rendering `run_kind` ([InsightsPage.tsx:395-460](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L395)), and the by-id envelope omits `run_kind` although the model declares it ([insights.py:819-826](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L819)): a hermetic probe returned `run_kind: null` for a replay row. The cutoff input carries no zone and the server reads it as UTC ([insights.py:115-116](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L115)), so `10:30` means 06:30 EDT (probe), and the input's `max` is the UTC clock ([InsightsPage.tsx:167](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L167)).
- **A replay is not fully point-in-time.** Three reads ignore the cutoff. The catalysts section's news slice runs 23 hours 59 minutes past it ([summarizers.py:1631](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1631)), so a probe with a 10:30 UTC cutoff captured a query ending at 10:29 UTC the next day. The reflection memory read takes no cutoff ([summarizers.py:1877](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1877)), though no row qualifies for it today. The trade planner's blue-sky offset comes from the newest calibration (`lib/strategies/exit_config_overrides.py:119-127`). The page describes the run as using data available at the chosen time ([InsightsPage.tsx:160](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L160)) (filed as [stocks#1217](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1217)).
- **Two cards can never fill.** Signal alerts are left out of the bundle on purpose ([summarizers.py:1963-1976](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1963)), so `supporting_signals` is always empty. Reflection memory needs rows with an `embedding` and `user_email IS NULL` ([summarizers.py:1902](../../lib/agents/summarizers.py#L1902)), while the journal route stamps every row with the caller's email or `local` (`platform/api/routers/journal.py:161`, `:705`) and writes no embedding, and the only embedding writer is the manual `scripts/backfill_journal_embeddings.py`. None of the 145 reports in the 60 days to 2026-09-28 carried either list (execution `db-query-jq7t2`), `journal_entries` held 2 rows with no embedding (execution `db-query-wqqwr`), and the cards present that design as an absence of data.
- **A trader or portfolio-manager failure publishes a fabricated plan with no banner.** The trader's fallback is flat with a zero entry and stop ([orchestrator.py:417-427](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L417)), the manager's carries confidence 0.3 ([orchestrator.py:467-482](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L467)), and neither adds a failed section. A hermetic render of such a report showed an entry of `0.00`, a stop of `0.00` and `Confidence 30%` with no degradation banner. No live report in the 60 days to 2026-09-28 had that shape (execution `db-query-zdb25`). When the strat section fails, the Strat card shows candle `1`, FTFC `0.00` and `mixed` from `_build_strat_snapshot` ([orchestrator.py:1102-1117](../../lib/agents/orchestrator.py#L1102), which also applies an unmarked `or 0.0` to `ftfc_score`).
- **Failed reads render as empty or as a permission problem.** History has no error branch, so a 503 reads `No history yet.` ([InsightsPage.tsx:404-417](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/InsightsPage.tsx#L404)). The Agents tab shows `Admin access required to view per-agent routing.` for any roster failure, a 503 for an admin included, and `Loading routing…` for an empty roster ([AgentsPanel.tsx:85-90](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/AgentsPanel.tsx#L85)), and a failed history there reads `No prior runs for` the ticker ([AgentsPanel.tsx:121-122](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/AgentsPanel.tsx#L121)). Hermetic renders on 2026-09-28 showed all three.
- **Chat.** The model is hard-coded ([insights.py:1042](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1042)) rather than read from `model_routing`. A Vertex failure is streamed as `Gemini error:` text with HTTP 200 ([insights.py:1058-1059](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1058), probe), which the page shows as the assistant's answer. The system prompts promise signals, backtests, GEX and playbook data ([insights.py:930-960](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L930)), and the request sends only the message, mode, ticker and six prior turns ([insights.py:1029-1037](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1029)). The route declares no `response_model` ([insights.py:1062](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L1062)), and the snapshot types its response as an empty schema.
- **The Watchlist tab is empty for every signed-in user.** The ranking is scoped to the caller's own rows ([insights.py:57-67](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L57)), and `watchlists` held rows for the `default` owner only on 2026-09-28 (one owner, 16 active rows, execution `db-query-wqqwr`). A hermetic probe of `rank_tickers` gave 0 candidates for a signed-in owner and 3 for `default`, and the empty copy blames the liquidity gate or empty catalyst tables ([WatchlistPanel.tsx:164-173](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/insights/WatchlistPanel.tsx#L164)). A database outage answers 200 with an empty ranking too (probe), because every ranker read goes through the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` (`lib/agents/ranker/candidates.py:60-63`, `lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:29-35`) and the watchlist load returns an empty list on failure (`gcp/fetchers/_watchlist.py:363-380`) (filed as [stocks#1215](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1215)).
- **The ranker's strat signal cannot score direction.** It compares `ftfc_direction` with `bull` and `bear` (`lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:74`), while the daily writer stores `bullish`, `bearish` or `mixed` (`lib/strat.py:332-336`), the only values a read-only query found in the last 30 days (execution `db-query-7fz78`). A probe scored a fully aligned `bullish` row 0.67 against 1.0 for `bull`, and the signal clamps a bearish row's negative `ftfc_score` to zero (`lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:73`). The ranker test feeds `bull` (`tests/agents/test_ranker.py:101`). The signal reads the newest row, which after 08:20 ET is the placeholder `fetch-premarket-refresh` inserts for the tickers it covers, and its NULL strat columns score as measured zeros (`lib/agents/ranker/signals.py:68-70`, probe) (filed as [stocks#1216](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1216)).
- No control removes a watchlist ticker: `useRemoveFromWatchlist` ([useTickerSearch.ts:137-147](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useTickerSearch.ts#L137)) has no caller. The add route's read-back of the list turns any error into an empty list ([insights.py:639-643](../../platform/api/routers/insights.py#L639)), unmarked.
- `insight-pipeline` carries the value of secret `admin-token` as a plain environment variable, `ADMIN_TOKEN` (`gcp/deploy.sh:575-576`), which the live job showed as a plain value on 2026-09-28 (tracked as [stocks#850](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/850)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for the report, history, by-id, run and chat routes, which run their own SQL on the connection from `lib/agents/model_routing.py` or call Vertex AI directly. 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md gives `insight-pipeline` 2Gi, `deploy_insight_pipeline` sets 8Gi and 2 CPU, and the live job runs 4Gi. UI-SCREENS.md lists only `/api/insights/chat` among the page's calls. The design seed says the Watchlist tab shows quotes and that chat talks with the report: the tab shows the ranker's ranking, and chat sends no report.
- `AUDIT-2026-05-13` markers on this page's paths: `lib/agents/summarizers.py:285` (a missing `price_vs_ema20` read as ranging), `lib/agents/trade_planner.py:874` (`or 0.0` on `atr`) and `:906` (`or 0.0` on `ftfc_score`). None sits in the router. Every route in the rows declares a `response_model` except the chat.
- No test was found for INSIGHTS-11 or INSIGHTS-14, and none exercises the page side of INSIGHTS-02, INSIGHTS-03, INSIGHTS-04, INSIGHTS-05, INSIGHTS-06, INSIGHTS-09, INSIGHTS-10 or INSIGHTS-13. [test_routers_insights_admin.py](../../tests/lib/test_routers_insights_admin.py) skips without a test Postgres, and its refresh test's fake `_sync_run` takes two arguments where the handler passes three: a hermetic probe under a strict test client raised `TypeError`.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page, earliest first (America/New_York): `fetch-news-sentiment-earnings` 06:00 Mon-Fri → `fetch-economic-events`, `fetch-insider-transactions` and the first `fetch-sec-filings` run 07:00 Mon-Fri → `fetch-news-sentiment` hourly from 08:00 and `fetch-news-sentiment-topics` hourly from 08:05, both to 17:00 or 17:05 Mon-Fri → `fetch-sec-filings` again at 10:00, 13:00 and 17:00 → `fetch-earnings-calendar` 19:00 Mon-Fri and Sun. All three news jobs run `gcp/fetchers/fetch_news_sentiment.py` and differ only in environment settings (`NEWS_TOPICS` for the topics job, `EARNINGS_WINDOW_DAYS` for the earnings job).
- Freshness the page assumes: the events query holds for five minutes and the types for an hour ([CatalystsPage.tsx:172](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L172), [CatalystsPage.tsx:184](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L184)), with no polling. The handler caches none of its SQL reads, and the news window is the 48 hours before each request ([catalysts.py:87](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L87)).
- How the handler tiers events decides what reaches Hot Now: every earnings row is `High` ([catalysts.py:475](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L475)), which makes every reporter due today or tomorrow a candidate for its ten slots, while an insider cluster is always `Medium` ([catalysts.py:519](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L519)) and never one.
- `GET /api/catalysts/events` also feeds DASHBOARD-06 and DASHBOARD-09, whose cells name four tables and one news writer. The rows here record the fifth read, `sec_filings` ([catalysts.py:526-534](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L526)), and every writer of each table.
- Producer tests: `fetch-economic-events` [test_fetch_economic_events.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_economic_events.py) · `fetch-earnings-calendar` [test_fetch_earnings_calendar_persist.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_earnings_calendar_persist.py), [test_fetch_earnings_calendar_yahoo.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_earnings_calendar_yahoo.py) · the news jobs [test_fetch_news_sentiment_args.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_news_sentiment_args.py), [test_fetch_news_sentiment_explode.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_news_sentiment_explode.py), [test_fetch_news_sentiment_incremental.py](../../tests/gcp/test_fetch_news_sentiment_incremental.py) · `fetch-sec-filings` [test_sec_filings_retry.py](../../tests/gcp/test_sec_filings_retry.py) · news, SEC and insider parsing [test_phase2_fetchers.py](../../tests/gcp/test_phase2_fetchers.py).

### Gaps
- [#863](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/863) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md. It concerns the weekly Discord post of `earnings_options_strategy_winners` and the `signal_metrics` classification. Neither table is on this page's path, which reads the five tables in CATALYSTS-04.
- **A database outage renders as a quiet range.** Each of the five reads runs through the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` (`gcp/database.py:239-241`) inside its own `except` ([catalysts.py:358-362](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L358) and its four siblings), so an outage answers 200 with no events and `source: Benzinga` (hermetic probe, 2026-09-28). [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) expects that 200.
- **Benzinga is named without Benzinga data.** Neither service sets `BENZINGA_API_KEY` (read 2026-09-28), and the image copies only `lib/`, `gcp/`, `scripts/` and `platform/api/` (`platform/Dockerfile:40-45`), so the cached Benzinga file is absent and requests call `_fetch_live_events`, which returns nothing without a key ([catalysts.py:138-140](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L138)). The response still names Benzinga, as the `AUDIT-2026-09-07` marker records ([catalysts.py:309-318](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L309)), and the page prints `Benzinga` when `source` is missing too ([CatalystsPage.tsx:488](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L488)): a hermetic render of a failed request showed `0 events` followed by `Benzinga` in the header. The Refresh button refetches with `refresh` false ([CatalystsPage.tsx:406](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L406)), so the page never asks for a Benzinga refresh (filed as [stocks#1223](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1223)).
- **News ignores the range and empties over weekends.** The range filters the Benzinga list before the database events are merged ([catalysts.py:276-289](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L276)), and the news query is bounded only by the 48 hours before the request ([catalysts.py:101-121](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L101)). A hermetic probe with an August range returned a 2026-09-25 article. The news jobs run Mon-Fri, and at 04:48 ET on Monday 2026-09-28 no row fell inside the window, the newest `published_ts` being 2026-09-25 18:26 UTC (execution `db-query-wqqwr`). News rows are dated `published_ts::date` ([catalysts.py:111](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L111)) in the database session's UTC zone, which the Signals area measured (area 08), so an article after 20:00 EDT is filed under the next day.
- `or 0` on financial fields, unmarked: news relevance and sentiment ([catalysts.py:394-395](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L394)), which also set the impact tier, and an insider cluster's count and value ([catalysts.py:508-509](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L508)). A read-only query found no NULL `sentiment_score` or `relevance_score` in the last 30 days (execution `db-query-8lfh7`).
- **No empty state and a fixed error text.** A range with no events shows only `0 events` and the filter bars (UI-SCREENS.md lists empty as absent). Any failed request reads `Failed to load catalysts: Failed to fetch catalysts`, because the hook throws one message whatever the status ([CatalystsPage.tsx:169](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L169)).
- Expanding an event un-truncates its title and nothing else ([CatalystsPage.tsx:278-292](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L278)). The URL, `actual`, `forecast`, `previous`, the insider value and the 8-K items the handler sends are typed on the page ([CatalystsPage.tsx:41-57](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L41)) and never rendered. `TYPE_CONFIG` has no `INSIDER_BUY` or `INSIDER_SELL` ([CatalystsPage.tsx:120-145](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L120)), so those badges and chips show the raw type ([CatalystsPage.tsx:212](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L212), [CatalystsPage.tsx:599](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/CatalystsPage.tsx#L599)).
- Secrets copied into plain environment variables on this page's producers: `fetch-earnings-calendar` carries secrets `ew-user` and `ew-pass` as `EW_USER` and `EW_PASS` (`gcp/deploy.sh:2767-2769`), and `fetch-sec-filings` carries `sec-user-agent` as `SEC_USER_AGENT` (`gcp/deploy.sh:2923-2924`). The live jobs showed all three as plain values, not secret references, on 2026-09-28 (tracked as [stocks#850](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/850)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md's Tables touched for `/api/catalysts/events` also omits `sec_filings` (its `news_sentiment` omission is in Dashboard's Gaps, area 04), and it gives `via lib/` for `/api/catalysts/types`, which returns two dictionaries defined in the router ([catalysts.py:50-78](../../platform/api/routers/catalysts.py#L50)). 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md lists `fetch-news-sentiment` and `fetch-news-sentiment-topics` as manual, and `gcp/deploy.sh` schedules both through `if _schedule_verified` entries (`news-sentiment-hourly`, `news-topics-hourly`, `gcp/deploy.sh:4985-4998`). The design seed puts `fetch-insider-transactions` under SEC EDGAR, and the fetcher calls AlphaVantage INSIDER_TRANSACTIONS (`gcp/fetchers/fetch_insider_transactions.py:41`, `:57`). It gives the earnings calendar three sources where `--source all` runs four, Yahoo included (`scripts/fetch_earnings_calendar.py:1479-1511`). The seed says an expanded event shows details, and UI-SCREENS.md and the seed both list a stale state the page does not have (CATALYSTS-13).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/catalysts.py`, and both routes declare a `response_model`.
- No test was found for CATALYSTS-06, CATALYSTS-08, CATALYSTS-10, CATALYSTS-11 or CATALYSTS-13, and none renders CATALYSTS-12's banner. The catalysts fixture exports `buildCatalystEventsEmpty`, which no spec uses.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page (America/New_York): `phase6-playbook` 04:30 Mon-Fri writes `playbook_cards` (`scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py --write-db`, [phase6_playbook.py:990](../../scripts/analysis/phase6_playbook.py#L990)) and also saves phase 6 markdown into the running container's own `reports/` directory (`scripts/analysis/shared_utils.py:328-334`), which nothing uploads. During the session the conditions evaluate against the live chain in PLAYBOOK-05, whose producers are area 05's.
- Freshness the page assumes: the card set holds for an hour and refetches every 15 minutes ([PlaybookPage.tsx:64-67](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L64)), the server caches it for an hour ([playbook.py:98-99](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L98)) and re-applies the seven-day limit ([playbook.py:82](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L82)) on every hit. A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found the newest `analysis_date` at 2026-09-28 for IWM, QQQ and SPY, 216 rows each (execution `db-query-jq7t2`).
- `GET /api/playbook/{ticker}` also feeds DASHBOARD-02, described the same way here.
- Producer tests: `phase6-playbook` [test_phase6_playbook.py](../../tests/scripts/test_phase6_playbook.py), [test_phase6_playbook_scheduler.py](../../tests/gcp/test_phase6_playbook_scheduler.py).

### Gaps
- UI-SCREENS.md still gives this page Status Broken and cites [#861](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/861) for it, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-07 by PR #1005, and `playbook_cards` is current (see Backend notes).
- **The empty state cannot appear.** A ticker without rows gets a 404 ([playbook.py:342-348](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L342)), which the page shows as the amber error: a hermetic render showed `Playbook unavailable for IWM: No playbook_cards rows for 'IWM'. Run the phase6-playbook Cloud Run job (--write-db).` The empty copy ([PlaybookPage.tsx:351-356](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L351)) needs a 200 with no cards, which only the spec's mock sends ([playbook.spec.ts:59-66](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts#L59)).
- **An evaluation failure reads as missing data.** Nothing reads the batch query's error, so every condition falls back to `no data` ([PlaybookPage.tsx:313-314](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L313), [PlaybookPage.tsx:363](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L363)), and the progress line labels every unknown condition `subjective` ([PlaybookPage.tsx:214-216](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L214)) whether the evaluator could not parse it or had no data. The percentage divides by every condition ([PlaybookPage.tsx:169](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L169)) while fully lit counts only the auto-evaluable ones ([PlaybookPage.tsx:172-173](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L172)).
- `useReference` returns `null` for any non-OK response ([PlaybookPage.tsx:77](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L77)), a data-access `return null` Rule 4 forbids, and the snapshot then carries no prior close, high or low without a message.
- The page cannot switch ticker itself (PLAYBOOK-04), as on Live (area 05).
- The spec's payload is an untyped literal ([playbook.spec.ts:9-28](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/playbook/playbook.spec.ts#L9)): its card has `direction` `long` and `win_rate` 0.62, while the page types direction as CALL, PUT or NEUTRAL ([PlaybookPage.tsx:26](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/PlaybookPage.tsx#L26)) and the handler sends win rates as percents ([playbook.py:251-252](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L251)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for both playbook routes, and the handler reads `playbook_cards` itself while the evaluator is inline in the router. 05-INFRASTRUCTURE.md's `manual` schedule for `phase6-playbook` is in Dashboard's Gaps (area 04). 05 also gives the job 8Gi, `deploy_phase6_playbook` sets 16Gi, 4 CPU and `--tasks 3 --parallelism 3` (`gcp/deploy.sh:1584-1607`), and the live job runs 16Gi, 4 CPU and 1 task (read 2026-09-28). The design seed gives it 1 GiB and `playbook tables`. UI-SCREENS.md lists only `/api/market/reference/` and `/api/playbook/` among the page's calls.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/playbook.py`, and every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- No test was found for PLAYBOOK-04 or PLAYBOOK-06. PLAYBOOK-07's spec test exercises a response the handler never sends.

### Backend notes
- No pipeline feeds this page. The 24 markdown objects under prefix `raw/reports/` were uploaded on 2026-04-12, and each one's generated line reads 2026-02-22 (the listing and copies read on 2026-09-28). The scripts that write such files save them in the running container (`scripts/analysis/shared_utils.py:328-334`), and no code or workflow in this repository uploads them.
- Freshness the page assumes: the list holds for 60 s and a body for five minutes ([ReportsPage.tsx:21](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L21), [ReportsPage.tsx:34](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ReportsPage.tsx#L34)). The server caches the list and each body for 24 hours ([playbook.py:100-103](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L100)) and the bucket listing for ten minutes (`platform/api/gcs_reader.py:72`).
- Order: the list is the ticker's own reports sorted descending by name, then the combined ones, descending too. For IWM a hermetic probe returned phase 6, 5, 4, 3, 2 and 1, then the multi-ticker 7, 6, 5d, 4 and 1. The page lands on the first entry and Previous and Next walk that order, while the dropdown groups phases ascending ([reports.ts:38-59](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/reports.ts#L38)).

### Gaps
- [#813](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/813) (open) is this page's blocking issue in UI-SCREENS.md. It concerns walk-forward calibration in `lib/walk_forward.py` and `scripts/run_param_sweep.py`, and nothing on this page reads either.
- **The first report and Previous or Next follow the server's reverse order.** A hermetic render with the real object names showed `Phase 6: Playbook` first, the counter at `1 / 11` and Previous disabled, while the dropdown listed phase 1 first. The spec's two-entry mock is already ascending, so its order test passes ([reports.spec.ts:55-68](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/reports/reports.spec.ts#L55)).
- **The empty state cannot appear, and a storage outage reads as not found.** An empty listing is a 404 ([playbook.py:429-433](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L429)), which the page shows as `Could not load the report list for` (hermetic render). The listing helper returns an empty list on any storage error (`platform/api/gcs_reader.py:93-95`), so an outage is the same 404, which [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) records. The spec's empty test mocks a 200 with an empty list ([reports.spec.ts:83-91](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/reports/reports.spec.ts#L83)).
- **Nothing shows a report's age.** The header shows the phase and filename only. The phase 6 report reads as generated on 2026-02-22, while the Playbook page's cards come from `playbook_cards` dated 2026-09-28.
- A ticker with no reports of its own gets the combined reports under its name: a hermetic probe for NVDA returned 200 with five combined phases, never the not-found state.
- `GET /api/reports/{ticker}/{phase}` is plain text with no `response_model` ([playbook.py:440](../../platform/api/routers/playbook.py#L440)), typed in the snapshot as a bare string.
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md describes the page as backtest, walk-forward and replay-trainer results and lists solyra's [replay-trainer.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/charts/replay-trainer.spec.ts) among its specs. The page serves the phase analysis markdown only. The design seed repeats that description and expects an empty list for a ticker without reports. 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for both routes, which read GCS through `platform/api/gcs_reader.py`.
- No `AUDIT` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/playbook.py` or `platform/api/gcs_reader.py`, whose swallowing listing CLAUDE.md §3.7.1 names.
- No test was found for REPORTS-06 or REPORTS-09. REPORTS-07's spec test exercises a response the handler never sends.

### Backend notes
- Pipeline order feeding this page (America/New_York): `signal-monitor` 09:25 Mon-Fri writes each live `trades` entry and exit and every live `signal_alerts` row through the session → `signal-monitor-eod-resolver` 16:30 Mon-Fri closes the trades left open → `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` 21:00 Mon-Sat and on the 1st, and `fetch-market-data` 23:00 Mon-Fri, write the chart's `market_data_intraday`. Users write `journal_entries`, here through marks, the form and imports and on the Charts page through its marks and replay sessions, and style mining writes `user_style_results` and `playbook_cards_staging`. `scripts/backfill_journal_embeddings.py`, run by hand, writes only `embedding` and `updated_at` on `journal_entries` (lines 78-83), neither of which this page reads.
- Freshness the page assumes: the own journal holds 10 s, and Examples 30 s with no retry ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:312-323](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L312), [useJournalChartTrades.ts:337-351](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L337)), every mutation refetches the ticker's journal and an import every ticker's, and the page shows no data age. A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found the newest live `trades` entries on 2026-09-25 for SPY and QQQ and on 2026-09-23 for IWM, with 989, 899 and 797 live rows inside regular hours, the rows Examples serve (execution `db-query-ld8s6`).
- Units: the page reads every `return_pct` as a percent. `signal-monitor` and its resolver write `trades.return_pct` as a percent of the underlying ([signal_monitor.py:2328-2331](../../gcp/signal_monitor.py#L2328), [signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py:87-94](../../gcp/signal_monitor_eod_resolver.py#L87)), marks and the manual form store a percent of the underlying ([journal.py:341-355](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L341)), and broker imports a percent of the option premium ([journal.py:358-372](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L358)). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found each of the 2,719 closed live `trades` rows in percent, the four that also fit a fraction being zero returns (execution `db-query-ld8s6`).
- `GET /api/journal/trades/{ticker}` also feeds CHARTS-02, `GET /api/market/dates/{ticker}` and `GET /api/market/data/{ticker}/{date}` carry CHARTS-01's and CHARTS-02's chains, and the picker is SIGNALS-01's and OPTIONS-06's, all described the same way here.
- Endpoints with no caller on this page: `GET /api/journal/seed/{ticker}` has a hook, `useSeedTrades` ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:475-491](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L475)), that no component calls, and `POST /api/backtest/replay-trades` is called from the Charts page, not this one.
- Producer tests for `signal-monitor` and its resolver sit in Signals' Backend notes (area 08).

### Gaps
- UI-SCREENS.md still cites [#717](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/717) for this page, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-11 as a duplicate of [#716](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/716). Item 1 of #716 (open), the mix of return units, still holds: `computeJournalStats` sums and averages every row's `return_pct` ([journalStats.ts:103-165](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/lib/journalStats.ts#L103)), and a broker import carries a percent of the option premium where every other row carries a percent of the underlying (Backend notes).
- **Examples multiply a percent by 100.** `_pipeline_rows_to_trades` multiplies `trades.return_pct` by 100 ([journal.py:531](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L531)) on the premise that it holds a fraction, and it holds a percent (Backend notes). A hermetic probe of the handler served a stored 0.13, a 600 to 600.78 CALL, as 13.0, and a render of the page showed `+13.00%` in the Return column. The same values drive the rail, the KPI tiles and the equity curve on the Examples view, which a new user sees first. The seed route multiplies the same way ([journal.py:1368](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1368)), and [test_journal_examples.py](../../tests/api/test_journal_examples.py) pins the premise with 0.01 stored for a 600 to 606 CALL and 1.0 expected ([test_journal_examples.py:612-620](../../tests/api/test_journal_examples.py#L612)) (filed as [stocks#1219](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1219)).
- **A failed own-journal read shows as local storage.** Nothing renders that query's error: the label falls back to `source ?? 'local'` ([JournalPage.tsx:223](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L223)) and reads `Local storage (set CLOUD_SQL_CONNECTION_NAME for persistence)`, and with no rows the view falls to Examples ([useJournalChartTrades.ts:362-367](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useJournalChartTrades.ts#L362)). A render with that read failing showed the label and no error text.
- **Failed marks, exits and deletes are silent.** Only the manual form's error renders ([JournalPage.tsx:494-498](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L494)). A chart mark posts and flips the view to My journal without waiting for the answer ([JournalPage.tsx:585-590](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L585)), and the exit and both delete buttons ([JournalPage.tsx:591](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L591), [JournalPage.tsx:628](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L628), [JournalPage.tsx:942](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L942)) render nothing on a failure (filed as [solyra#76](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/76)).
- **A delete that matches nothing reports success.** The handler ignores the row count and answers `deleted` ([journal.py:1257-1263](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1257)), which a hermetic probe confirmed with a count of 0 (filed as [stocks#1220](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1220)).
- **Export to Pipeline writes a file nothing reads.** The handler writes `{ticker}_trade_tracker.csv` into `data/signals/` on the serving instance ([journal.py:1376-1422](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1376)), where a hermetic probe saw it land, and no code in `gcp/`, `lib/`, `scripts/` or `platform/` names that file. The page reports the count on success, and any failure, a 422 included, becomes `API unavailable: downloaded CSV locally` ([JournalPage.tsx:330-343](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L330)).
- **Style mining reads a Cloud SQL outage as too few trades.** The trade read goes through the swallowing `query_to_dataframe` (`_replay_journal_query`, [backtest.py:486-490](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L486)), so an outage answers 200 `{"status": "unavailable", "reason": "need >= 10 closed trades, have 0"}` (hermetic probe), while the handler's docstring promises a 5xx ([backtest.py:699-700](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L699)) and [test_route_coverage.py](../../tests/api/test_route_coverage.py) expects 200 with no storage. The six months of bars come through `lib/data_loader.py`, whose SQL wrapper answers empty on any error ([data_loader.py:78-114](../../lib/data_loader.py#L78)) and whose `load_best_available` then returns daily bars ([data_loader.py:498-519](../../lib/data_loader.py#L498)): a hermetic probe with the intraday query failing got the daily bars back, and the response does not say which kind the profile was validated on.
- **`staged` reaches nothing.** The two inserts go to `user_style_results` and `playbook_cards_staging` ([backtest.py:843-903](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L843)), 05-c lists no reader of either, `PLAYBOOK_USER_CARDS` is `False` ([backtest.py:613](../../platform/api/routers/backtest.py#L613)), and both tables were empty on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-ld8s6`). The badge's tooltip says the profile `was staged server-side for signal evaluation` ([MyStylePanel.tsx:118-126](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/journal/MyStylePanel.tsx#L118)).
- `POST /api/journal/import/preview` is declared `async def` ([journal.py:1431](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1431)) and makes the blocking `_existing_entry_keys` read inside it ([journal.py:1504](../../platform/api/routers/journal.py#L1504)), the shape CLAUDE.md §3.10 describes.
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md lists only `/api/journal/export/` and `/api/journal/trades` among the page's calls, where the rows trace fourteen, and gives the component 945 lines, where it has 970. The design seed lists the export route as a GET, where the page posts, and `/api/analytics/summary` and `trade-stats`, which the page never calls. Its chart journey clicks the entry bar, then the exit bar, then CALL or PUT, where the page takes the entry, then CALL or PUT, then optional targets and a stop, and the exit later from the rail card ([JournalPage.tsx:386-433](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/JournalPage.tsx#L386)). 04-BACKEND-API.md gives `via lib/` for the create, export and import routes, which do their own SQL, file or `lib/broker_import.py` work. solyra's CLAUDE.md names [TradeMarkingChart.tsx:202](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/journal/TradeMarkingChart.tsx#L202) as the existing `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker, where that line sits in a comment on the drawing-step effect, and the one marker in solyra is at [ChartsPage.tsx:364](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/ChartsPage.tsx#L364) (area 06).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/journal.py` or `platform/api/routers/backtest.py`, and the one in `lib/data_loader.py` (line 660) is in `load_trades`, off this path. Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- No test was found for JOURNAL-12 or JOURNAL-15, and no spec saves the manual form or clicks `CSV` or `Export to Pipeline`: JOURNAL-08's and JOURNAL-10's tests are backend and helper tests only.

### Backend notes
- No scheduled pipeline feeds this page's own tables: `user_roles`, `model_routing` and `admin_refresh_leases` are written from it (ADMIN-04, ADMIN-07, ADMIN-06), and `model_routing` starts from seed rows, though its seven rows read on 2026-09-28 carry `updated_by` `claude-code/ab-test-restore-2026-05-12`, not the page's `admin-ui` (execution `db-query-jq7t2`). The Data tab reads the freshness audit over tables whose producers 05-c lists. No scheduled trigger writes the GCS objects behind the models tab: `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_train.py` writes the per-cell artifacts through a manual dispatch of `strat-engine`, and `gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_ece_snapshot.py` writes the live-ECE snapshot on demand. The predict's features come from `strat-engine` at 23:35 ET Mon-Fri and its levels tables at 02:00 ET Tue-Sat.
- Freshness the page assumes: users, sources and routes hold 30 s, models five minutes, and the brief and the state 60 s ([useAdmin.ts:47](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L47), [useAdmin.ts:65](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L65), [useAdmin.ts:122](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L122), [useAdmin.ts:183](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L183), [useAdmin.ts:281](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L281), [useAdmin.ts:349](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L349)), nothing polls, and each mutation refetches its list. The server caches the freshness audit for five minutes ([health.py:42](../../platform/api/routers/health.py#L42)).
- Access: the page decides from `/api/me`'s `is_admin` alone ([AdminPage.tsx:36-48](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/AdminPage.tsx#L36)), and every admin route checks again through `_require_admin`. On prod, where `AUTH_MODE` is `iap`, the identity is the IAP header, and a Firebase account's status does not govern access there (ADMIN-05).
- `POST /api/admin/strat-engine/structure-continuation` ([admin.py:735](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L735)) has no caller in solyra, and `STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION_ENABLED` is set on neither service (read 2026-09-28), so it answers 404.
- `GET /api/admin/routes` also feeds INSIGHTS-02, described the same way here.
- The refresh registry maps its nine refreshable datasets to seven jobs, and each job grants `roles/run.invoker` to `trading-platform-svc@` (read 2026-09-28). `admin_refresh_leases` held no row on 2026-09-28 (execution `db-query-ld8s6`), and no code deletes its rows, so no refresh has been accepted since the table was created.

### Gaps
- UI-SCREENS.md still cites [#838](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/838) for this page, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-14 after access became role-based (PRs #956 and #959), and the page takes no token ([AdminPage.tsx:27-33](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/AdminPage.tsx#L27)). The leftover `ADMIN_TOKEN` on `insight-pipeline` is tracked in [#850](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/850) (open) and recorded in the Insights Gaps (area 09).
- **Every Structure Brief cell is unavailable.** `research/strat_engine/structure_brief_latest.json` does not exist (listing read 2026-09-28), so all nine cells carry the deploy-gate note. A storage failure gives the same note, since `_load_structure_brief_snapshot` returns None on any error ([admin.py:262-283](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L262)): a hermetic probe with the storage client failing got 200 with nine unavailable cells.
- **A storage outage reads as an empty shelf.** `_strat_engine_state_cells` returns an empty list when the client fails and marks a cell unavailable on any read error ([admin.py:486-495](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L486), [admin.py:516-522](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L516)). A hermetic probe with the client failing got 200 `{"cells": [], "ece_ceiling": 0.05}`, and a render of that answer read `0 / 0 models trained · 0 muted · ECE ceiling 0.050 · no live ECE snapshot yet`. Nothing flags the served models' age: the nine `model.pkl` objects date from 2026-05-26 for IWM and 2026-06-04 for SPY and QQQ (listing read 2026-09-28).
- **The intraday refresh reason is wrong, and the page never shows it.** The registry says `market_data_intraday` has no on-demand refresh because its `only writer is the monthly bulk backfill` ([admin.py:1263-1264](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1263)), while `gcp/deploy.sh` schedules `fetch-market-data` each weeknight and `fetch-alphavantage-intraday` every night from Monday to Saturday besides the monthly run (CHARTS-01). The page disables the button with a generic tooltip ([DataSourcesPanel.tsx:117-120](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/admin/DataSourcesPanel.tsx#L117)), so the reason reaches only an API caller: a hermetic probe got it with a 409.
- **The report's own `stale` flag is dropped.** The handler passes on `stale` and `stale_age_seconds` ([admin.py:1418-1425](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1418)), and the page's response type holds only `sources` ([useAdmin.ts:340-342](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useAdmin.ts#L340)), so a cached report handed out during another audit shows as current, the unread-field shape CLAUDE.md §3.7.1 describes. `coverage_start` is always null ([admin.py:1384](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1384)).
- **Disable cannot work on prod.** Prod runs `AUTH_MODE=iap` (read 2026-09-28), where the handler answers 409 ([admin.py:1158-1166](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1158)), and the page shows Disable and Enable for every account ([UsersPanel.tsx:149-163](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/admin/UsersPanel.tsx#L149)) (filed as [solyra#78](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/78)).
- **Granting a role to an account that holds one fails.** `toggleRole` sends the account's current roles plus the clicked one ([UsersPanel.tsx:45-48](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/admin/UsersPanel.tsx#L45)), and the server keeps one role per account and answers 422 for two ([admin.py:1083-1087](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L1083)): a hermetic probe sending `["user", "admin"]` got 422 `an account holds one role — send [], ["user"], or ["admin"]`, a message that also leaves out `dev`. The spec toggles a role-less account only ([admin-tabs.spec.ts:74-89](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/admin/admin-tabs.spec.ts#L74)), while [test_admin_users_datasources.py](../../tests/api/test_admin_users_datasources.py) pins the 422 (filed as [solyra#77](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/77)).
- **A role-store outage reads as a missing role.** `/api/me` reports no stored role when the `user_roles` lookup fails ([auth.py:229-264](../../platform/api/auth.py#L229)), so an admin granted through the table gets `Admin access required`: a hermetic probe with the lookup failing answered 200 with `is_admin` false. The `ADMIN_EMAIL` account keeps access, the fallback the code documents.
- Class probabilities: the server pads the classes a model lacks with 0.0 ([strat_pred_serve.py:442-443](../../gcp/research/strat_engine/strat_pred_serve.py#L442)), and both displays turn a missing class into 0 with an unmarked `?? 0` ([StructureBrief.tsx:318](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/structure_brief/StructureBrief.tsx#L318), [PredictForm.tsx:183](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/structure_brief/PredictForm.tsx#L183)).
- The model list offers `gemini-2.0-flash` with credentials, which the schema's own comment says 404s against the adapter's default `global` location ([schema.sql:1527-1531](../../gcp/schema.sql#L1527)), and `set_route` checks only the price table and the adapter ([model_routing.py:164-206](../../lib/agents/model_routing.py#L164)). Not checked against Vertex (filed as [stocks#1221](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1221)).
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md gives the component 369 lines, where it has 344, lists only `/api/admin/routes` among its calls, where the rows trace twelve, and marks the empty and stale states absent, while the page renders an empty search, an empty category and unavailable cells (ADMIN-10) but no age (ADMIN-12). 04-BACKEND-API.md's admin inventory holds seven of the router's twelve routes, without users, roles, status, data sources and refresh, and describes `/api/admin/models` as loading the structure-brief snapshot, where it lists priced models. The routing panel's comment says the roles seed on `vertex:gemini-2.0-flash` ([AdminPage.tsx:157-168](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/AdminPage.tsx#L157)), and the schema seeds `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` (filed as [stocks#1221](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/1221)).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/admin.py`, `platform/api/auth.py` or the admin components, though `_load_structure_brief_snapshot` and `_strat_engine_state_cells` swallow storage errors (above). Every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- No test was found for ADMIN-09, and ADMIN-08's tests are backend only: no spec runs a predict.

### Backend notes
- No pipeline feeds this page. `user_preferences` and `user_profile` are written only from it, besides the header's theme toggle, which writes preferences through the same stores (SHELL-10), and the Role line's `user_roles` comes from the Admin page (ADMIN-04). A read-only query on 2026-09-28 found one `user_preferences` row and no `user_profile` rows (execution `db-query-ld8s6`).
- Freshness the page assumes: preferences and the profile hold five minutes with one retry ([usePreferences.ts:147-151](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L147), [useProfile.ts:96-101](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useProfile.ts#L96)) and `/api/me` 30 s ([useUser.ts:75](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useUser.ts#L75)). The stored appearance applies once per session.
- Two persistence models by design ([SettingsPage.tsx:1-13](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SettingsPage.tsx#L1)): appearance applies at once in `localStorage` (`platform-theme`, `platform-shell-settings`) and writes through, and the other tabs are a draft saved with `Save changes`, sending the changed fields only.
- `PUT /api/me/preferences` also carries SHELL-10's header toggle, and `GET /api/me` the shell's auth status (SHELL-04, SHELL-16), described the same way here.

### Gaps
- **UI-SCREENS.md describes an older page** (spec §14). It gives the purpose as device-local appearance preferences, finds no API calls, and counts 131 lines and no E2E spec, where the page syncs appearance through `/api/me/preferences` and saves a profile through `/api/me/profile` (SETTINGS-01 to SETTINGS-07), has 492 lines, and has [settings.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/settings/settings.spec.ts). It also cites [#685](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/stocks/issues/685) for the page, but that issue was closed on 2026-09-03 as not planned, and it concerns the Heatseeker and Flowseeker names (moved to solyra [#27](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/issues/27), also closed), not this page.
- **Nothing reads the profile.** No solyra module besides `useProfile` and this page reads a `user_profile` field, and 05-c lists the router as the table's only reader. The copy promises uses that do not exist: a time zone `Used for session clocks and event times.`, defaults for the charts, options and signals pages, sizing that pre-fills `in the journal and playbook`, and email alerts to the sign-in address, with no sender anywhere.
- **Any failure, a defect included, is a 503.** Both routers catch every exception on read and write ([preferences.py:137-141](../../platform/api/routers/preferences.py#L137), [preferences.py:189-193](../../platform/api/routers/preferences.py#L189), [profile.py:150-154](../../platform/api/routers/profile.py#L150), [profile.py:201-205](../../platform/api/routers/profile.py#L201)), so a programming error reads as an outage: a hermetic probe raising a `TypeError` in the row read got 503 `preferences temporarily unavailable` and `profile temporarily unavailable`, where the admin router checks `is_infrastructure_error` first ([admin.py:147-153](../../platform/api/routers/admin.py#L147)).
- **The page cannot show staleness.** The stores take the stored appearance once per session ([usePreferences.ts:159-189](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L159)) and later reads do not re-apply it, and the header says `Synced to your account.` with no time ([SettingsPage.tsx:191-192](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/SettingsPage.tsx#L191)).
- The spec mocks the nothing-stored reads as a 200 with nulls and an empty object ([settings.spec.ts:22-50](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/settings/settings.spec.ts#L22)), while both routers answer 404 ([preferences.py:142-145](../../platform/api/routers/preferences.py#L142), [profile.py:155-158](../../platform/api/routers/profile.py#L155)), which the hooks read as nothing stored ([usePreferences.ts:92-98](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/usePreferences.ts#L92), [useProfile.ts:76-81](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useProfile.ts#L76)).
- The design seed gives 11 accent swatches, where the page has 12 ([settingsStore.ts:20-33](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/stores/settingsStore.ts#L20)), reports zero E2E coverage, and has a journey where saved sizing pre-fills the journal and playbook, which nothing does (above).
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in either router or on the page's path, and every route in the rows declares a `response_model`.
- No test was found for SETTINGS-08, SETTINGS-09 or SETTINGS-12, or for `Discard`. SETTINGS-03, SETTINGS-04 and SETTINGS-05 have backend tests only.

### Backend notes
- No pipeline feeds this page. Its one request, `GET /api/config/indicators`, answers from literals in the handler and `load_config()`, which reads `alert_config.json` from the working directory ([config.py:704-718](../../lib/config.py#L704)). The API image copies no such file ([Dockerfile:40-45](../../platform/Dockerfile#L40)), so the service answers with the `AppConfig` defaults (the running image was not inspected).
- Freshness the page assumes: the config holds 24 hours ([useConfig.ts:55-66](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/hooks/useConfig.ts#L55)), and the entries are written into the page.
- `GET /api/glossary/gamma` ([glossary.py:30](../../platform/api/routers/glossary.py#L30)) serves `lib/gamma_glossary.py`'s public dictionary and has no caller in solyra.

### Gaps
- **A failed config read shows fallback thresholds without a word.** `buildGlossary` substitutes fallbacks while the config is missing ([HelpPage.tsx:17-25](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L17)), and nothing reads the query's error, so a failed read renders StochRSI as `Below 20 = oversold. Above 80 = overbought.`, where the server says 30 and 70 ([config.py:435-436](../../lib/config.py#L435)): a render with the read failing showed that text and no error. The comment above calls the fallbacks the documented defaults from `lib/config.py` ([HelpPage.tsx:12-15](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L12)).
- The shared mock payload, which says it mirrors the production config, sets StochRSI 20 and 80 and `min_conditions` 7 ([help.ts:48-49](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/help.ts#L48)), where the server's are 30, 70 and 3, and its fan-out lists a `<WatchlistPanel>` the page does not mount ([help.ts:8-10](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/mocks/help.ts#L8)). [help.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/help/help.spec.ts) renders against that mock.
- `/api/config/indicators` returns literals for part of its answer: RSI 30 and 70, the five RSI zones, `atr.high_threshold` 2.0 and `rvol.signal_threshold` 1.0 ([config.py:85-114](../../platform/api/routers/config.py#L85)), not values from `load_config`, so the glossary's `requires RVOL > 1.0` comes from the handler rather than the signal engine's gate, which reads `rvol_gate_min` and `rvol_gate_mode` (`gcp/signal_monitor.py:1428-1431`). The answer's `call_range` and `put_range`, which no page renders, come from `SignalConfig` defaults, 25 to 50 and 50 to 75 ([config.py:427-428](../../lib/config.py#L427)): the API image copies no `alert_config.json`, the jobs' image does (`gcp/Dockerfile:23`), and that file sets 45 to 70 and 30 to 55 (the running images were not inspected).
- **No TermHover exists.** The Options Gamma Map's `Term` spans show a `title` tooltip from the mock `glossary` in [gammaMapMock.ts:255](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/data/gammaMapMock.ts#L255) ([SwingMode.tsx:129-138](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L129)), not this page's entries or `GET /api/glossary/gamma`, and its `Glossary` button opens `/help` without a term or an anchor ([SwingMode.tsx:307-315](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/components/options/SwingMode.tsx#L307)). UI-SCREENS.md credits TermHover to PR #546, the design seed's journey follows a hovered term into the glossary, and the glossary router's docstring still names `<TermHover>` and HelpPage as its consumers ([glossary.py:1-12](../../platform/api/routers/glossary.py#L1)).
- `GET /api/glossary/gamma` has no caller and no `response_model` ([glossary.py:30](../../platform/api/routers/glossary.py#L30)).
- **The glossary is written by hand.** The 131 entries in 11 categories are literals in the page ([HelpPage.tsx:16-190](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/src/routes/HelpPage.tsx#L16)), six of whose details take config values, and the gamma entries are kept apart from `lib/gamma_glossary.py`, the dictionary the router serves.
- Sources that disagree with the code: UI-SCREENS.md gives the page 296 lines, where it has 295. The design seed says the page makes no API calls, where `useIndicatorConfig` makes one, names a `STRAT` pill, where the pill reads `The Strat`, and expands a `King level` entry, where the entry is `King Node (★)`.
- No `AUDIT-2026-05-13` marker sits in `platform/api/routers/config.py`, `platform/api/routers/glossary.py` or the page.
- No test was found for HELP-04 or HELP-07. [help.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/help/help.spec.ts) checks the heading, the input's presence and a load budget. [gamma-levels.spec.ts](https://github.com/TeneikaAskew/solyra/blob/main/tests/shared/gamma-levels.spec.ts) covers HELP-02, HELP-05 and HELP-06 with the Gamma Levels and The Strat pills and one search term, `King Node`.
